{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_bdrk_01TKJDRsY4JRGvJ8PKav6Pxm","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_bdrk_01QhyxmADEexZsdDhn2j5Fo3","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/solve_acopf.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nAC Optimal Power Flow Solver for ISO Base Case Analysis\nUses CasADi + IPOPT to solve ACOPF based on complex-number formulation\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom casadi import *\n\ndef load_network_data(filename):\n    \"\"\"Load and parse MATPOWER-format network data\"\"\"\n    with open(filename, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n\n    # Parse bus data\n    buses = {}\n    for row in data['bus']:\n        bus_id = int(row[0])\n        buses[bus_id] = {\n            'id': bus_id,\n            'type': int(row[1]),  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref\n            'pd': row[2] / baseMVA,  # MW -> pu\n            'qd': row[3] / baseMVA,  # MVAr -> pu\n            'gs': row[4] / baseMVA,  # shunt conductance\n            'bs': row[5] / baseMVA,  # shunt susceptance\n            'vm': row[7],  # voltage magnitude\n            'va': row[8],  # voltage angle (degrees)\n            'vmin': row[11],\n            'vmax': row[12],\n        }\n\n    # Parse generator data\n    generators = []\n    for idx, row in enumerate(data['gen']):\n        bus_id = int(row[0])\n        gen = {\n            'id': idx,\n            'bus': bus_id,\n            'pg': row[1] / baseMVA,  # MW -> pu\n            'qg': row[2] / baseMVA,  # MVAr -> pu\n            'qmax': row[3] / baseMVA,\n            'qmin': row[4] / baseMVA,\n            'vg': row[5],\n            'mbase': row[6],\n            'status': int(row[7]),\n            'pmax': row[8] / baseMVA,\n            'pmin': row[9] / baseMVA,\n        }\n        if gen['status'] == 1:\n            generators.append(gen)\n\n    # Parse generator cost data\n    for idx, cost_row in enumerate(data['gencost']):\n        if idx < len(generators):\n            model = int(cost_row[0])  # 1=piecewise, 2=polynomial\n            ncost = int(cost_row[3])\n            if model == 2 and ncost == 3:\n                # Quadratic cost: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\n                generators[idx]['c2'] = cost_row[4] * baseMVA**2\n                generators[idx]['c1'] = cost_row[5] * baseMVA\n                generators[idx]['c0'] = cost_row[6]\n            else:\n                # Default to linear\n                generators[idx]['c2'] = 0.0\n                generators[idx]['c1'] = cost_row[-2] * baseMVA if len(cost_row) > 5 else 1000.0\n                generators[idx]['c0'] = cost_row[-1] if len(cost_row) > 6 else 0.0\n\n    # Parse branch data\n    branches = []\n    for row in data['branch']:\n        fbus = int(row[0])\n        tbus = int(row[1])\n\n        # Branch impedance and admittance\n        r = row[2]\n        x = row[3]\n        b = row[4]  # total line charging susceptance\n\n        if abs(r) < 1e-10 and abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            # Zero impedance - skip or handle specially\n            continue\n\n        # Series admittance\n        z = r + 1j*x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        # Shunt admittances (pi model)\n        yc_from = 1j * b / 2.0\n        yc_to = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Transformer tap ratio and phase shift\n        ratio = row[8] if abs(row[8]) > 1e-6 else 1.0\n        angle = row[9] * np.pi / 180.0  # degrees to radians\n        tap = ratio * np.exp(1j * angle)\n\n        # Rating\n        rateA = row[5] / baseMVA if row[5] > 0 else 9900.0\n\n        branch = {\n            'from': fbus,\n            'to': tbus,\n            'y': y,  # series admittance\n            'yc_from': yc_from,\n            'yc_to': yc_to,\n            'tap': tap,\n            'rateA': rateA,\n            'status': int(row[10]),\n        }\n\n        if branch['status'] == 1:\n            branches.append(branch)\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'generators': generators,\n        'branches': branches,\n    }\n\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve AC Optimal Power Flow using CasADi\"\"\"\n\n    buses = network['buses']\n    generators = network['generators']\n    branches = network['branches']\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    # Bus indices\n    bus_ids = sorted(buses.keys())\n    bus_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n    n_bus = len(bus_ids)\n    n_gen = len(generators)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    print(f\"Setting up ACOPF: {n_bus} buses, {n_gen} generators, {n_branch} branches\")\n\n    # Decision variables\n    # For each bus: Vr, Vi (real and imaginary voltage)\n    # For each gen: Pg, Qg\n    V_r = MX.sym('Vr', n_bus)\n    V_i = MX.sym('Vi', n_bus)\n    P_g = MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)\n    Q_g = MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)\n\n    # Stack all variables\n    x = vertcat(V_r, V_i, P_g, Q_g)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    obj = 0\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        c2 = gen.get('c2', 0.0)\n        c1 = gen.get('c1', 1000.0)\n        c0 = gen.get('c0', 0.0)\n        pg_mw = P_g[i] * baseMVA\n        obj += c2 * pg_mw**2 + c1 * pg_mw + c0\n\n    # Constraints\n    g = []  # equality constraints\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []  # inequality constraints\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Build generator mapping\n    gen_at_bus = {bid: [] for bid in bus_ids}\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        gen_at_bus[gen['bus']].append(i)\n\n    # Power balance equations (complex form)\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n\n        V_complex = V_r[i] + 1j * V_i[i]\n        V_mag_sq = V_r[i]**2 + V_i[i]**2\n\n        # Net generation at this bus\n        P_gen = sum(P_g[gi] for gi in gen_at_bus[bid]) if gen_at_bus[bid] else 0\n        Q_gen = sum(Q_g[gi] for gi in gen_at_bus[bid]) if gen_at_bus[bid] else 0\n\n        # Load at this bus\n        P_load = bus['pd']\n        Q_load = bus['qd']\n\n        # Shunt injection\n        P_shunt = bus['gs'] * V_mag_sq\n        Q_shunt = -bus['bs'] * V_mag_sq\n\n        # Branch flows from this bus\n        P_branch = 0\n        Q_branch = 0\n\n        for br in branches:\n            if br['from'] == bid:\n                # Forward flow\n                fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n                ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n                V_f = V_r[fi] + 1j * V_i[fi]\n                V_t = V_r[ti] + 1j * V_i[ti]\n\n                y = br['y']\n                yc_f = br['yc_from']\n                tap = br['tap']\n                tap_mag_sq = (tap.real**2 + tap.imag**2)\n\n                # S_ij = (y + yc_f)* |Vf|^2 / |tap|^2 - y* Vf Vt* / tap\n                # Using complex arithmetic in CasADi\n                V_f_r = V_r[fi]\n                V_f_i = V_i[fi]\n                V_t_r = V_r[ti]\n                V_t_i = V_i[ti]\n\n                # (y + yc_f)* |Vf|^2 / |tap|^2\n                y_tot = y + yc_f\n                y_tot_conj_r = y_tot.real\n                y_tot_conj_i = -y_tot.imag\n                V_mag_sq_f = V_f_r**2 + V_f_i**2\n                term1_r = y_tot_conj_r * V_mag_sq_f / tap_mag_sq\n                term1_i = y_tot_conj_i * V_mag_sq_f / tap_mag_sq\n\n                # -y* Vf Vt* / tap\n                y_conj_r = y.real\n                y_conj_i = -y.imag\n                tap_r = tap.real\n                tap_i = tap.imag\n\n                # Vf * Vt* = (Vf_r + j*Vf_i)(Vt_r - j*Vt_i)\n                VfVt_conj_r = V_f_r * V_t_r + V_f_i * V_t_i\n                VfVt_conj_i = V_f_i * V_t_r - V_f_r * V_t_i\n\n                # y* * (Vf*Vt*)\n                prod_r = y_conj_r * VfVt_conj_r - y_conj_i * VfVt_conj_i\n                prod_i = y_conj_r * VfVt_conj_i + y_conj_i * VfVt_conj_r\n\n                # / tap = / (tap_r + j*tap_i)\n                tap_mag_sq_br = tap_r**2 + tap_i**2\n                term2_r = (prod_r * tap_r + prod_i * tap_i) / tap_mag_sq_br\n                term2_i = (prod_i * tap_r - prod_r * tap_i) / tap_mag_sq_br\n\n                S_ij_r = term1_r - term2_r\n                S_ij_i = term1_i - term2_i\n\n                P_branch += S_ij_r\n                Q_branch += S_ij_i\n\n            if br['to'] == bid:\n                # Reverse flow\n                fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n                ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n                V_f = V_r[fi] + 1j * V_i[fi]\n                V_t = V_r[ti] + 1j * V_i[ti]\n\n                y = br['y']\n                yc_t = br['yc_to']\n                tap = br['tap']\n\n                V_f_r = V_r[fi]\n                V_f_i = V_i[fi]\n                V_t_r = V_r[ti]\n                V_t_i = V_i[ti]\n\n                # S_ji = (y + yc_t)* |Vt|^2 - y* Vf* Vt / tap*\n                y_tot = y + yc_t\n                y_tot_conj_r = y_tot.real\n                y_tot_conj_i = -y_tot.imag\n                V_mag_sq_t = V_t_r**2 + V_t_i**2\n                term1_r = y_tot_conj_r * V_mag_sq_t\n                term1_i = y_tot_conj_i * V_mag_sq_t\n\n                # y* Vf* Vt / tap*\n                y_conj_r = y.real\n                y_conj_i = -y.imag\n                tap_conj_r = tap.real\n                tap_conj_i = -tap.imag\n\n                # Vf* * Vt = (Vf_r - j*Vf_i)(Vt_r + j*Vt_i)\n                VfVt_r = V_f_r * V_t_r + V_f_i * V_t_i\n                VfVt_i = V_f_i * V_t_r - V_f_r * V_t_i\n                # Wait, let me recalculate\n                VfVt_i = -V_f_i * V_t_r + V_f_r * V_t_i\n\n                # Actually: Vf* Vt = (Vf_r - j Vf_i)(Vt_r + j Vt_i)\n                #                   = Vf_r*Vt_r + j*Vf_r*Vt_i - j*Vf_i*Vt_r + Vf_i*Vt_i\n                VfconjVt_r = V_f_r * V_t_r + V_f_i * V_t_i\n                VfconjVt_i = V_f_r * V_t_i - V_f_i * V_t_r\n\n                # y* * (Vf* Vt)\n                prod_r = y_conj_r * VfconjVt_r - y_conj_i * VfconjVt_i\n                prod_i = y_conj_r * VfconjVt_i + y_conj_i * VfconjVt_r\n\n                # / tap* = / (tap_conj_r + j*tap_conj_i)\n                tap_conj_mag_sq = tap_conj_r**2 + tap_conj_i**2\n                term2_r = (prod_r * tap_conj_r + prod_i * tap_conj_i) / tap_conj_mag_sq\n                term2_i = (prod_i * tap_conj_r - prod_r * tap_conj_i) / tap_conj_mag_sq\n\n                S_ji_r = term1_r - term2_r\n                S_ji_i = term1_i - term2_i\n\n                P_branch += S_ji_r\n                Q_branch += S_ji_i\n\n        # Power balance: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = P_branch\n        g.append(P_gen - P_load - P_shunt - P_branch)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n        # Q balance\n        g.append(Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt - Q_branch)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Reference bus angle constraint\n    ref_buses = [bid for bid in bus_ids if buses[bid]['type'] == 3]\n    if ref_buses:\n        ref_idx = bus_idx[ref_buses[0]]\n        g.append(V_i[ref_idx])  # Va = 0 means Vi = 0 (Vr = |V|)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Voltage magnitude limits\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        V_mag_sq = V_r[i]**2 + V_i[i]**2\n        h.append(V_mag_sq)\n        lbh.append(bus['vmin']**2)\n        ubh.append(bus['vmax']**2)\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    for br in branches:\n        fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n        ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n\n        # Forward flow S_ij\n        V_f_r = V_r[fi]\n        V_f_i = V_i[fi]\n        V_t_r = V_r[ti]\n        V_t_i = V_i[ti]\n\n        y = br['y']\n        yc_f = br['yc_from']\n        tap = br['tap']\n        tap_mag_sq = (tap.real**2 + tap.imag**2)\n\n        y_tot = y + yc_f\n        y_tot_conj_r = y_tot.real\n        y_tot_conj_i = -y_tot.imag\n        V_mag_sq_f = V_f_r**2 + V_f_i**2\n        term1_r = y_tot_conj_r * V_mag_sq_f / tap_mag_sq\n        term1_i = y_tot_conj_i * V_mag_sq_f / tap_mag_sq\n\n        y_conj_r = y.real\n        y_conj_i = -y.imag\n        tap_r = tap.real\n        tap_i = tap.imag\n\n        VfVt_conj_r = V_f_r * V_t_r + V_f_i * V_t_i\n        VfVt_conj_i = V_f_i * V_t_r - V_f_r * V_t_i\n\n        prod_r = y_conj_r * VfVt_conj_r - y_conj_i * VfVt_conj_i\n        prod_i = y_conj_r * VfVt_conj_i + y_conj_i * VfVt_conj_r\n\n        tap_mag_sq_br = tap_r**2 + tap_i**2\n        term2_r = (prod_r * tap_r + prod_i * tap_i) / tap_mag_sq_br\n        term2_i = (prod_i * tap_r - prod_r * tap_i) / tap_mag_sq_br\n\n        S_ij_r = term1_r - term2_r\n        S_ij_i = term1_i - term2_i\n\n        S_ij_mag_sq = S_ij_r**2 + S_ij_i**2\n        h.append(S_ij_mag_sq)\n        lbh.append(0)\n        ubh.append(br['rateA']**2)\n\n        # Reverse flow S_ji\n        y_tot = y + br['yc_to']\n        y_tot_conj_r = y_tot.real\n        y_tot_conj_i = -y_tot.imag\n        V_mag_sq_t = V_t_r**2 + V_t_i**2\n        term1_r = y_tot_conj_r * V_mag_sq_t\n        term1_i = y_tot_conj_i * V_mag_sq_t\n\n        VfconjVt_r = V_f_r * V_t_r + V_f_i * V_t_i\n        VfconjVt_i = V_f_r * V_t_i - V_f_i * V_t_r\n\n        prod_r = y_conj_r * VfconjVt_r - y_conj_i * VfconjVt_i\n        prod_i = y_conj_r * VfconjVt_i + y_conj_i * VfconjVt_r\n\n        tap_conj_r = tap.real\n        tap_conj_i = -tap.imag\n        tap_conj_mag_sq = tap_conj_r**2 + tap_conj_i**2\n        term2_r = (prod_r * tap_conj_r + prod_i * tap_conj_i) / tap_conj_mag_sq\n        term2_i = (prod_i * tap_conj_r - prod_r * tap_conj_i) / tap_conj_mag_sq\n\n        S_ji_r = term1_r - term2_r\n        S_ji_i = term1_i - term2_i\n\n        S_ji_mag_sq = S_ji_r**2 + S_ji_i**2\n        h.append(S_ji_mag_sq)\n        lbh.append(0)\n        ubh.append(br['rateA']**2)\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n\n    # Voltage real/imag bounds (approximate as [-vmax, vmax])\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        lbx.append(-bus['vmax'])\n        ubx.append(bus['vmax'])\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        lbx.append(-bus['vmax'])\n        ubx.append(bus['vmax'])\n\n    # Generator P bounds\n    for gen in generators:\n        lbx.append(gen['pmin'])\n        ubx.append(gen['pmax'])\n\n    # Generator Q bounds\n    for gen in generators:\n        lbx.append(gen['qmin'])\n        ubx.append(gen['qmax'])\n\n    # Initial guess\n    x0 = []\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        v0 = bus['vm']\n        a0 = bus['va'] * np.pi / 180.0\n        x0.append(v0 * np.cos(a0))  # Vr\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        v0 = bus['vm']\n        a0 = bus['va'] * np.pi / 180.0\n        x0.append(v0 * np.sin(a0))  # Vi\n    for gen in generators:\n        x0.append(gen['pg'])\n    for gen in generators:\n        x0.append(gen['qg'])\n\n    # Setup NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': obj,\n        'g': vertcat(*g, *h) if (g and h) else (vertcat(*g) if g else vertcat(*h))\n    }\n\n    lbg_all = lbg + lbh\n    ubg_all = ubg + ubh\n\n    # Solver options\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'print_time': 0,\n    }\n\n    solver = nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving ACOPF...\")\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg_all, ubg=ubg_all)\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n\n    V_r_opt = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n    V_i_opt = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    P_g_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Q_g_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    # Compute voltage magnitudes and angles\n    V_mag = np.sqrt(V_r_opt**2 + V_i_opt**2)\n    V_ang = np.arctan2(V_i_opt, V_r_opt) * 180 / np.pi\n\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    success = stats['success']\n\n    result = {\n        'success': success,\n        'solver_status': 'optimal' if success else 'infeasible',\n        'objective': float(sol['f']),\n        'bus_idx': bus_idx,\n        'V_mag': V_mag,\n        'V_ang': V_ang,\n        'P_g': P_g_opt,\n        'Q_g': Q_g_opt,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n    }\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef generate_report(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Generate the final report.json\"\"\"\n\n    buses = network['buses']\n    generators = network['generators']\n    branches = network['branches']\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    bus_idx = solution['bus_idx']\n    V_mag = solution['V_mag']\n    V_ang = solution['V_ang']\n    P_g = solution['P_g']\n    Q_g = solution['Q_g']\n\n    # Total generation\n    total_gen_mw = sum(P_g) * baseMVA\n    total_gen_mvar = sum(Q_g) * baseMVA\n\n    # Total load\n    total_load_mw = sum(b['pd'] for b in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n    total_load_mvar = sum(b['qd'] for b in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n\n    # Total losses\n    total_losses_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n    # Generator report\n    gen_report = []\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        gen_report.append({\n            'id': gen['id'],\n            'bus': gen['bus'],\n            'pg_MW': float(P_g[i] * baseMVA),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Q_g[i] * baseMVA),\n            'pmin_MW': float(gen['pmin'] * baseMVA),\n            'pmax_MW': float(gen['pmax'] * baseMVA),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(gen['qmin'] * baseMVA),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(gen['qmax'] * baseMVA),\n        })\n\n    # Bus report\n    bus_report = []\n    for bid in sorted(buses.keys()):\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        bus_report.append({\n            'id': bid,\n            'vm_pu': float(V_mag[i]),\n            'va_deg': float(V_ang[i]),\n            'vmin_pu': float(bus['vmin']),\n            'vmax_pu': float(bus['vmax']),\n        })\n\n    # Branch flows and loading\n    branch_loadings = []\n    for br in branches:\n        fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n        ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n\n        V_f = V_mag[fi] * np.exp(1j * V_ang[fi] * np.pi / 180)\n        V_t = V_mag[ti] * np.exp(1j * V_ang[ti] * np.pi / 180)\n\n        y = br['y']\n        yc_f = br['yc_from']\n        yc_t = br['yc_to']\n        tap = br['tap']\n\n        # Forward flow\n        S_ij = np.conj(y + yc_f) * abs(V_f)**2 / abs(tap)**2 - np.conj(y) * V_f * np.conj(V_t) / tap\n        # Reverse flow\n        S_ji = np.conj(y + yc_t) * abs(V_t)**2 - np.conj(y) * np.conj(V_f) * V_t / np.conj(tap)\n\n        flow_from_mva = abs(S_ij) * baseMVA\n        flow_to_mva = abs(S_ji) * baseMVA\n        limit_mva = br['rateA'] * baseMVA\n\n        loading_pct = max(flow_from_mva, flow_to_mva) / limit_mva * 100 if limit_mva > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_loadings.append({\n            'from_bus': br['from'],\n            'to_bus': br['to'],\n            'loading_pct': float(loading_pct),\n            'flow_from_MVA': float(flow_from_mva),\n            'flow_to_MVA': float(flow_to_mva),\n            'limit_MVA': float(limit_mva),\n        })\n\n    # Most loaded branches (top 10)\n    branch_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_loadings[:10]\n\n    # Feasibility check - compute mismatches\n    max_p_mismatch = 0.0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0.0\n\n    gen_at_bus = {}\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        if gen['bus'] not in gen_at_bus:\n            gen_at_bus[gen['bus']] = []\n        gen_at_bus[gen['bus']].append(i)\n\n    for bid in buses.keys():\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n\n        V_complex = V_mag[i] * np.exp(1j * V_ang[i] * np.pi / 180)\n\n        # Generation\n        P_gen = sum(P_g[gi] for gi in gen_at_bus.get(bid, []))\n        Q_gen = sum(Q_g[gi] for gi in gen_at_bus.get(bid, []))\n\n        # Load and shunt\n        P_load = bus['pd']\n        Q_load = bus['qd']\n        P_shunt = bus['gs'] * abs(V_complex)**2\n        Q_shunt = -bus['bs'] * abs(V_complex)**2\n\n        # Branch flows\n        P_branch = 0\n        Q_branch = 0\n\n        for br in branches:\n            if br['from'] == bid:\n                fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n                ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n                V_f = V_mag[fi] * np.exp(1j * V_ang[fi] * np.pi / 180)\n                V_t = V_mag[ti] * np.exp(1j * V_ang[ti] * np.pi / 180)\n                y = br['y']\n                yc_f = br['yc_from']\n                tap = br['tap']\n                S_ij = np.conj(y + yc_f) * abs(V_f)**2 / abs(tap)**2 - np.conj(y) * V_f * np.conj(V_t) / tap\n                P_branch += S_ij.real\n                Q_branch += S_ij.imag\n\n            if br['to'] == bid:\n                fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n                ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n                V_f = V_mag[fi] * np.exp(1j * V_ang[fi] * np.pi / 180)\n                V_t = V_mag[ti] * np.exp(1j * V_ang[ti] * np.pi / 180)\n                y = br['y']\n                yc_t = br['yc_to']\n                tap = br['tap']\n                S_ji = np.conj(y + yc_t) * abs(V_t)**2 - np.conj(y) * np.conj(V_f) * V_t / np.conj(tap)\n                P_branch += S_ji.real\n                Q_branch += S_ji.imag\n\n        p_mismatch = abs(P_gen - P_load - P_shunt - P_branch)\n        q_mismatch = abs(Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt - Q_branch)\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n    # Voltage violations\n    max_v_violation = 0.0\n    for bid in buses.keys():\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        if V_mag[i] < bus['vmin']:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, bus['vmin'] - V_mag[i])\n        elif V_mag[i] > bus['vmax']:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, V_mag[i] - bus['vmax'])\n\n    # Branch overloads\n    max_overload = 0.0\n    for bl in branch_loadings:\n        overload = max(bl['flow_from_MVA'], bl['flow_to_MVA']) - bl['limit_MVA']\n        if overload > 0:\n            max_overload = max(max_overload, overload)\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(solution['objective']),\n            'total_load_MW': float(total_load_mw),\n            'total_load_MVAr': float(total_load_mvar),\n            'total_generation_MW': float(total_gen_mw),\n            'total_generation_MVAr': float(total_gen_mvar),\n            'total_losses_MW': float(total_losses_mw),\n            'solver_status': solution['solver_status'],\n        },\n        'generators': gen_report,\n        'buses': bus_report,\n        'most_loaded_branches': most_loaded,\n        'feasibility_check': {\n            'max_p_mismatch_MW': float(max_p_mismatch * baseMVA),\n            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': float(max_q_mismatch * baseMVA),\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_v_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_overload),\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(\"AC Optimal Power Flow - Base Case Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    # Solve ACOPF\n    solution = solve_acopf(network)\n\n    if not solution['success']:\n        print(\"\\nWARNING: Solver did not converge to optimal solution\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\nACOPF solved successfully!\")\n        print(f\"Total cost: ${solution['objective']:,.2f}/hour\")\n\n    # {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_bdrk_01QhyxmADEexZsdDhn2j5Fo3","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /root/solve_acopf.py"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"3b33a68c-e002-43bd-b0bc-83ce40604541","uuid":"2c045481-9e39-4b8c-94ba-afbbab2dcdc8","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/root/solve_acopf.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nAC Optimal Power Flow Solver for ISO Base Case Analysis\nUses CasADi + IPOPT to solve ACOPF based on complex-number formulation\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom casadi import *\n\ndef load_network_data(filename):\n    \"\"\"Load and parse MATPOWER-format network data\"\"\"\n    with open(filename, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n\n    # Parse bus data\n    buses = {}\n    for row in data['bus']:\n        bus_id = int(row[0])\n        buses[bus_id] = {\n            'id': bus_id,\n            'type': int(row[1]),  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref\n            'pd': row[2] / baseMVA,  # MW -> pu\n            'qd': row[3] / baseMVA,  # MVAr -> pu\n            'gs': row[4] / baseMVA,  # shunt conductance\n            'bs': row[5] / baseMVA,  # shunt susceptance\n            'vm': row[7],  # voltage magnitude\n            'va': row[8],  # voltage angle (degrees)\n            'vmin': row[11],\n            'vmax': row[12],\n        }\n\n    # Parse generator data\n    generators = []\n    for idx, row in enumerate(data['gen']):\n        bus_id = int(row[0])\n        gen = {\n            'id': idx,\n            'bus': bus_id,\n            'pg': row[1] / baseMVA,  # MW -> pu\n            'qg': row[2] / baseMVA,  # MVAr -> pu\n            'qmax': row[3] / baseMVA,\n            'qmin': row[4] / baseMVA,\n            'vg': row[5],\n            'mbase': row[6],\n            'status': int(row[7]),\n            'pmax': row[8] / baseMVA,\n            'pmin': row[9] / baseMVA,\n        }\n        if gen['status'] == 1:\n            generators.append(gen)\n\n    # Parse generator cost data\n    for idx, cost_row in enumerate(data['gencost']):\n        if idx < len(generators):\n            model = int(cost_row[0])  # 1=piecewise, 2=polynomial\n            ncost = int(cost_row[3])\n            if model == 2 and ncost == 3:\n                # Quadratic cost: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\n                generators[idx]['c2'] = cost_row[4] * baseMVA**2\n                generators[idx]['c1'] = cost_row[5] * baseMVA\n                generators[idx]['c0'] = cost_row[6]\n            else:\n                # Default to linear\n                generators[idx]['c2'] = 0.0\n                generators[idx]['c1'] = cost_row[-2] * baseMVA if len(cost_row) > 5 else 1000.0\n                generators[idx]['c0'] = cost_row[-1] if len(cost_row) > 6 else 0.0\n\n    # Parse branch data\n    branches = []\n    for row in data['branch']:\n        fbus = int(row[0])\n        tbus = int(row[1])\n\n        # Branch impedance and admittance\n        r = row[2]\n        x = row[3]\n        b = row[4]  # total line charging susceptance\n\n        if abs(r) < 1e-10 and abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            # Zero impedance - skip or handle specially\n            continue\n\n        # Series admittance\n        z = r + 1j*x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        # Shunt admittances (pi model)\n        yc_from = 1j * b / 2.0\n        yc_to = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Transformer tap ratio and phase shift\n        ratio = row[8] if abs(row[8]) > 1e-6 else 1.0\n        angle = row[9] * np.pi / 180.0  # degrees to radians\n        tap = ratio * np.exp(1j * angle)\n\n        # Rating\n        rateA = row[5] / baseMVA if row[5] > 0 else 9900.0\n\n        branch = {\n            'from': fbus,\n            'to': tbus,\n            'y': y,  # series admittance\n            'yc_from': yc_from,\n            'yc_to': yc_to,\n            'tap': tap,\n            'rateA': rateA,\n            'status': int(row[10]),\n        }\n\n        if branch['status'] == 1:\n            branches.append(branch)\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'generators': generators,\n        'branches': branches,\n    }\n\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve AC Optimal Power Flow using CasADi\"\"\"\n\n    buses = network['buses']\n    generators = network['generators']\n    branches = network['branches']\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    # Bus indices\n    bus_ids = sorted(buses.keys())\n    bus_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n    n_bus = len(bus_ids)\n    n_gen = len(generators)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    print(f\"Setting up ACOPF: {n_bus} buses, {n_gen} generators, {n_branch} branches\")\n\n    # Decision variables\n    # For each bus: Vr, Vi (real and imaginary voltage)\n    # For each gen: Pg, Qg\n    V_r = MX.sym('Vr', n_bus)\n    V_i = MX.sym('Vi', n_bus)\n    P_g = MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)\n    Q_g = MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)\n\n    # Stack all variables\n    x = vertcat(V_r, V_i, P_g, Q_g)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    obj = 0\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        c2 = gen.get('c2', 0.0)\n        c1 = gen.get('c1', 1000.0)\n        c0 = gen.get('c0', 0.0)\n        pg_mw = P_g[i] * baseMVA\n        obj += c2 * pg_mw**2 + c1 * pg_mw + c0\n\n    # Constraints\n    g = []  # equality constraints\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []  # inequality constraints\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Build generator mapping\n    gen_at_bus = {bid: [] for bid in bus_ids}\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        gen_at_bus[gen['bus']].append(i)\n\n    # Power balance equations (complex form)\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n\n        V_complex = V_r[i] + 1j * V_i[i]\n        V_mag_sq = V_r[i]**2 + V_i[i]**2\n\n        # Net generation at this bus\n        P_gen = sum(P_g[gi] for gi in gen_at_bus[bid]) if gen_at_bus[bid] else 0\n        Q_gen = sum(Q_g[gi] for gi in gen_at_bus[bid]) if gen_at_bus[bid] else 0\n\n        # Load at this bus\n        P_load = bus['pd']\n        Q_load = bus['qd']\n\n        # Shunt injection\n        P_shunt = bus['gs'] * V_mag_sq\n        Q_shunt = -bus['bs'] * V_mag_sq\n\n        # Branch flows from this bus\n        P_branch = 0\n        Q_branch = 0\n\n        for br in branches:\n            if br['from'] == bid:\n                # Forward flow\n                fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n                ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n                V_f = V_r[fi] + 1j * V_i[fi]\n                V_t = V_r[ti] + 1j * V_i[ti]\n\n                y = br['y']\n                yc_f = br['yc_from']\n                tap = br['tap']\n                tap_mag_sq = (tap.real**2 + tap.imag**2)\n\n                # S_ij = (y + yc_f)* |Vf|^2 / |tap|^2 - y* Vf Vt* / tap\n                # Using complex arithmetic in CasADi\n                V_f_r = V_r[fi]\n                V_f_i = V_i[fi]\n                V_t_r = V_r[ti]\n                V_t_i = V_i[ti]\n\n                # (y + yc_f)* |Vf|^2 / |tap|^2\n                y_tot = y + yc_f\n                y_tot_conj_r = y_tot.real\n                y_tot_conj_i = -y_tot.imag\n                V_mag_sq_f = V_f_r**2 + V_f_i**2\n                term1_r = y_tot_conj_r * V_mag_sq_f / tap_mag_sq\n                term1_i = y_tot_conj_i * V_mag_sq_f / tap_mag_sq\n\n                # -y* Vf Vt* / tap\n                y_conj_r = y.real\n                y_conj_i = -y.imag\n                tap_r = tap.real\n                tap_i = tap.imag\n\n                # Vf * Vt* = (Vf_r + j*Vf_i)(Vt_r - j*Vt_i)\n                VfVt_conj_r = V_f_r * V_t_r + V_f_i * V_t_i\n                VfVt_conj_i = V_f_i * V_t_r - V_f_r * V_t_i\n\n                # y* * (Vf*Vt*)\n                prod_r = y_conj_r * VfVt_conj_r - y_conj_i * VfVt_conj_i\n                prod_i = y_conj_r * VfVt_conj_i + y_conj_i * VfVt_conj_r\n\n                # / tap = / (tap_r + j*tap_i)\n                tap_mag_sq_br = tap_r**2 + tap_i**2\n                term2_r = (prod_r * tap_r + prod_i * tap_i) / tap_mag_sq_br\n                term2_i = (prod_i * tap_r - prod_r * tap_i) / tap_mag_sq_br\n\n                S_ij_r = term1_r - term2_r\n                S_ij_i = term1_i - term2_i\n\n                P_branch += S_ij_r\n                Q_branch += S_ij_i\n\n            if br['to'] == bid:\n                # Reverse flow\n                fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n                ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n                V_f = V_r[fi] + 1j * V_i[fi]\n                V_t = V_r[ti] + 1j * V_i[ti]\n\n                y = br['y']\n                yc_t = br['yc_to']\n                tap = br['tap']\n\n                V_f_r = V_r[fi]\n                V_f_i = V_i[fi]\n                V_t_r = V_r[ti]\n                V_t_i = V_i[ti]\n\n                # S_ji = (y + yc_t)* |Vt|^2 - y* Vf* Vt / tap*\n                y_tot = y + yc_t\n                y_tot_conj_r = y_tot.real\n                y_tot_conj_i = -y_tot.imag\n                V_mag_sq_t = V_t_r**2 + V_t_i**2\n                term1_r = y_tot_conj_r * V_mag_sq_t\n                term1_i = y_tot_conj_i * V_mag_sq_t\n\n                # y* Vf* Vt / tap*\n                y_conj_r = y.real\n                y_conj_i = -y.imag\n                tap_conj_r = tap.real\n                tap_conj_i = -tap.imag\n\n                # Vf* * Vt = (Vf_r - j*Vf_i)(Vt_r + j*Vt_i)\n                VfVt_r = V_f_r * V_t_r + V_f_i * V_t_i\n                VfVt_i = V_f_i * V_t_r - V_f_r * V_t_i\n                # Wait, let me recalculate\n                VfVt_i = -V_f_i * V_t_r + V_f_r * V_t_i\n\n                # Actually: Vf* Vt = (Vf_r - j Vf_i)(Vt_r + j Vt_i)\n                #                   = Vf_r*Vt_r + j*Vf_r*Vt_i - j*Vf_i*Vt_r + Vf_i*Vt_i\n                VfconjVt_r = V_f_r * V_t_r + V_f_i * V_t_i\n                VfconjVt_i = V_f_r * V_t_i - V_f_i * V_t_r\n\n                # y* * (Vf* Vt)\n                prod_r = y_conj_r * VfconjVt_r - y_conj_i * VfconjVt_i\n                prod_i = y_conj_r * VfconjVt_i + y_conj_i * VfconjVt_r\n\n                # / tap* = / (tap_conj_r + j*tap_conj_i)\n                tap_conj_mag_sq = tap_conj_r**2 + tap_conj_i**2\n                term2_r = (prod_r * tap_conj_r + prod_i * tap_conj_i) / tap_conj_mag_sq\n                term2_i = (prod_i * tap_conj_r - prod_r * tap_conj_i) / tap_conj_mag_sq\n\n                S_ji_r = term1_r - term2_r\n                S_ji_i = term1_i - term2_i\n\n                P_branch += S_ji_r\n                Q_branch += S_ji_i\n\n        # Power balance: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = P_branch\n        g.append(P_gen - P_load - P_shunt - P_branch)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n        # Q balance\n        g.append(Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt - Q_branch)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Reference bus angle constraint\n    ref_buses = [bid for bid in bus_ids if buses[bid]['type'] == 3]\n    if ref_buses:\n        ref_idx = bus_idx[ref_buses[0]]\n        g.append(V_i[ref_idx])  # Va = 0 means Vi = 0 (Vr = |V|)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Voltage magnitude limits\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        V_mag_sq = V_r[i]**2 + V_i[i]**2\n        h.append(V_mag_sq)\n        lbh.append(bus['vmin']**2)\n        ubh.append(bus['vmax']**2)\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    for br in branches:\n        fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n        ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n\n        # Forward flow S_ij\n        V_f_r = V_r[fi]\n        V_f_i = V_i[fi]\n        V_t_r = V_r[ti]\n        V_t_i = V_i[ti]\n\n        y = br['y']\n        yc_f = br['yc_from']\n        tap = br['tap']\n        tap_mag_sq = (tap.real**2 + tap.imag**2)\n\n        y_tot = y + yc_f\n        y_tot_conj_r = y_tot.real\n        y_tot_conj_i = -y_tot.imag\n        V_mag_sq_f = V_f_r**2 + V_f_i**2\n        term1_r = y_tot_conj_r * V_mag_sq_f / tap_mag_sq\n        term1_i = y_tot_conj_i * V_mag_sq_f / tap_mag_sq\n\n        y_conj_r = y.real\n        y_conj_i = -y.imag\n        tap_r = tap.real\n        tap_i = tap.imag\n\n        VfVt_conj_r = V_f_r * V_t_r + V_f_i * V_t_i\n        VfVt_conj_i = V_f_i * V_t_r - V_f_r * V_t_i\n\n        prod_r = y_conj_r * VfVt_conj_r - y_conj_i * VfVt_conj_i\n        prod_i = y_conj_r * VfVt_conj_i + y_conj_i * VfVt_conj_r\n\n        tap_mag_sq_br = tap_r**2 + tap_i**2\n        term2_r = (prod_r * tap_r + prod_i * tap_i) / tap_mag_sq_br\n        term2_i = (prod_i * tap_r - prod_r * tap_i) / tap_mag_sq_br\n\n        S_ij_r = term1_r - term2_r\n        S_ij_i = term1_i - term2_i\n\n        S_ij_mag_sq = S_ij_r**2 + S_ij_i**2\n        h.append(S_ij_mag_sq)\n        lbh.append(0)\n        ubh.append(br['rateA']**2)\n\n        # Reverse flow S_ji\n        y_tot = y + br['yc_to']\n        y_tot_conj_r = y_tot.real\n        y_tot_conj_i = -y_tot.imag\n        V_mag_sq_t = V_t_r**2 + V_t_i**2\n        term1_r = y_tot_conj_r * V_mag_sq_t\n        term1_i = y_tot_conj_i * V_mag_sq_t\n\n        VfconjVt_r = V_f_r * V_t_r + V_f_i * V_t_i\n        VfconjVt_i = V_f_r * V_t_i - V_f_i * V_t_r\n\n        prod_r = y_conj_r * VfconjVt_r - y_conj_i * VfconjVt_i\n        prod_i = y_conj_r * VfconjVt_i + y_conj_i * VfconjVt_r\n\n        tap_conj_r = tap.real\n        tap_conj_i = -tap.imag\n        tap_conj_mag_sq = tap_conj_r**2 + tap_conj_i**2\n        term2_r = (prod_r * tap_conj_r + prod_i * tap_conj_i) / tap_conj_mag_sq\n        term2_i = (prod_i * tap_conj_r - prod_r * tap_conj_i) / tap_conj_mag_sq\n\n        S_ji_r = term1_r - term2_r\n        S_ji_i = term1_i - term2_i\n\n        S_ji_mag_sq = S_ji_r**2 + S_ji_i**2\n        h.append(S_ji_mag_sq)\n        lbh.append(0)\n        ubh.append(br['rateA']**2)\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n\n    # Voltage real/imag bounds (approximate as [-vmax, vmax])\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        lbx.append(-bus['vmax'])\n        ubx.append(bus['vmax'])\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        lbx.append(-bus['vmax'])\n        ubx.append(bus['vmax'])\n\n    # Generator P bounds\n    for gen in generators:\n        lbx.append(gen['pmin'])\n        ubx.append(gen['pmax'])\n\n    # Generator Q bounds\n    for gen in generators:\n        lbx.append(gen['qmin'])\n        ubx.append(gen['qmax'])\n\n    # Initial guess\n    x0 = []\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        v0 = bus['vm']\n        a0 = bus['va'] * np.pi / 180.0\n        x0.append(v0 * np.cos(a0))  # Vr\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        v0 = bus['vm']\n        a0 = bus['va'] * np.pi / 180.0\n        x0.append(v0 * np.sin(a0))  # Vi\n    for gen in generators:\n        x0.append(gen['pg'])\n    for gen in generators:\n        x0.append(gen['qg'])\n\n    # Setup NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': obj,\n        'g': vertcat(*g, *h) if (g and h) else (vertcat(*g) if g else vertcat(*h))\n    }\n\n    lbg_all = lbg + lbh\n    ubg_all = ubg + ubh\n\n    # Solver options\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'print_time': 0,\n    }\n\n    solver = nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving ACOPF...\")\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg_all, ubg=ubg_all)\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n\n    V_r_opt = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n    V_i_opt = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    P_g_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Q_g_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    # Compute voltage magnitudes and angles\n    V_mag = np.sqrt(V_r_opt**2 + V_i_opt**2)\n    V_ang = np.arctan2(V_i_opt, V_r_opt) * 180 / np.pi\n\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    success = stats['success']\n\n    result = {\n        'success': success,\n        'solver_status': 'optimal' if success else 'infeasible',\n        'objective': float(sol['f']),\n        'bus_idx': bus_idx,\n        'V_mag': V_mag,\n        'V_ang': V_ang,\n        'P_g': P_g_opt,\n        'Q_g': Q_g_opt,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n    }\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef generate_report(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Generate the final report.json\"\"\"\n\n    buses = network['buses']\n    generators = network['generators']\n    branches = network['branches']\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    bus_idx = solution['bus_idx']\n    V_mag = solution['V_mag']\n    V_ang = solution['V_ang']\n    P_g = solution['P_g']\n    Q_g = solution['Q_g']\n\n    # Total generation\n    total_gen_mw = sum(P_g) * baseMVA\n    total_gen_mvar = sum(Q_g) * baseMVA\n\n    # Total load\n    total_load_mw = sum(b['pd'] for b in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n    total_load_mvar = sum(b['qd'] for b in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n\n    # Total losses\n    total_losses_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n    # Generator report\n    gen_report = []\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        gen_report.append({\n            'id': gen['id'],\n            'bus': gen['bus'],\n            'pg_MW': float(P_g[i] * baseMVA),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Q_g[i] * baseMVA),\n            'pmin_MW': float(gen['pmin'] * baseMVA),\n            'pmax_MW': float(gen['pmax'] * baseMVA),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(gen['qmin'] * baseMVA),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(gen['qmax'] * baseMVA),\n        })\n\n    # Bus report\n    bus_report = []\n    for bid in sorted(buses.keys()):\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        bus_report.append({\n            'id': bid,\n            'vm_pu': float(V_mag[i]),\n            'va_deg': float(V_ang[i]),\n            'vmin_pu': float(bus['vmin']),\n            'vmax_pu': float(bus['vmax']),\n        })\n\n    # Branch flows and loading\n    branch_loadings = []\n    for br in branches:\n        fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n        ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n\n        V_f = V_mag[fi] * np.exp(1j * V_ang[fi] * np.pi / 180)\n        V_t = V_mag[ti] * np.exp(1j * V_ang[ti] * np.pi / 180)\n\n        y = br['y']\n        yc_f = br['yc_from']\n        yc_t = br['yc_to']\n        tap = br['tap']\n\n        # Forward flow\n        S_ij = np.conj(y + yc_f) * abs(V_f)**2 / abs(tap)**2 - np.conj(y) * V_f * np.conj(V_t) / tap\n        # Reverse flow\n        S_ji = np.conj(y + yc_t) * abs(V_t)**2 - np.conj(y) * np.conj(V_f) * V_t / np.conj(tap)\n\n        flow_from_mva = abs(S_ij) * baseMVA\n        flow_to_mva = abs(S_ji) * baseMVA\n        limit_mva = br['rateA'] * baseMVA\n\n        loading_pct = max(flow_from_mva, flow_to_mva) / limit_mva * 100 if limit_mva > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_loadings.append({\n            'from_bus': br['from'],\n            'to_bus': br['to'],\n            'loading_pct': float(loading_pct),\n            'flow_from_MVA': float(flow_from_mva),\n            'flow_to_MVA': float(flow_to_mva),\n            'limit_MVA': float(limit_mva),\n        })\n\n    # Most loaded branches (top 10)\n    branch_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_loadings[:10]\n\n    # Feasibility check - compute mismatches\n    max_p_mismatch = 0.0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0.0\n\n    gen_at_bus = {}\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        if gen['bus'] not in gen_at_bus:\n            gen_at_bus[gen['bus']] = []\n        gen_at_bus[gen['bus']].append(i)\n\n    for bid in buses.keys():\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n\n        V_complex = V_mag[i] * np.exp(1j * V_ang[i] * np.pi / 180)\n\n        # Generation\n        P_gen = sum(P_g[gi] for gi in gen_at_bus.get(bid, []))\n        Q_gen = sum(Q_g[gi] for gi in gen_at_bus.get(bid, []))\n\n        # Load and shunt\n        P_load = bus['pd']\n        Q_load = bus['qd']\n        P_shunt = bus['gs'] * abs(V_complex)**2\n        Q_shunt = -bus['bs'] * abs(V_complex)**2\n\n        # Branch flows\n        P_branch = 0\n        Q_branch = 0\n\n        for br in branches:\n            if br['from'] == bid:\n                fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n                ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n                V_f = V_mag[fi] * np.exp(1j * V_ang[fi] * np.pi / 180)\n                V_t = V_mag[ti] * np.exp(1j * V_ang[ti] * np.pi / 180)\n                y = br['y']\n                yc_f = br['yc_from']\n                tap = br['tap']\n                S_ij = np.conj(y + yc_f) * abs(V_f)**2 / abs(tap)**2 - np.conj(y) * V_f * np.conj(V_t) / tap\n                P_branch += S_ij.real\n                Q_branch += S_ij.imag\n\n            if br['to'] == bid:\n                fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n                ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n                V_f = V_mag[fi] * np.exp(1j * V_ang[fi] * np.pi / 180)\n                V_t = V_mag[ti] * np.exp(1j * V_ang[ti] * np.pi / 180)\n                y = br['y']\n                yc_t = br['yc_to']\n                tap = br['tap']\n                S_ji = np.conj(y + yc_t) * abs(V_t)**2 - np.conj(y) * np.conj(V_f) * V_t / np.conj(tap)\n                P_branch += S_ji.real\n                Q_branch += S_ji.imag\n\n        p_mismatch = abs(P_gen - P_load - P_shunt - P_branch)\n        q_mismatch = abs(Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt - Q_branch)\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n    # Voltage violations\n    max_v_violation = 0.0\n    for bid in buses.keys():\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        if V_mag[i] < bus['vmin']:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, bus['vmin'] - V_mag[i])\n        elif V_mag[i] > bus['vmax']:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, V_mag[i] - bus['vmax'])\n\n    # Branch overloads\n    max_overload = 0.0\n    for bl in branch_loadings:\n        overload = max(bl['flow_from_MVA'], bl['flow_to_MVA']) - bl['limit_MVA']\n        if overload > 0:\n            max_overload = max(max_overload, overload)\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(solution['objective']),\n            'total_load_MW': float(total_load_mw),\n            'total_load_MVAr': float(total_load_mvar),\n            'total_generation_MW': float(total_gen_mw),\n            'total_generation_MVAr': float(total_gen_mvar),\n            'total_losses_MW': float(total_losses_mw),\n            'solver_status': solution['solver_status'],\n        },\n        'generators': gen_report,\n        'buses': bus_report,\n        'most_loaded_branches': most_loaded,\n        'feasibility_check': {\n            'max_p_mismatch_MW': float(max_p_mismatch * baseMVA),\n            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': float(max_q_mismatch * baseMVA),\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_v_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_overload),\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(\"AC Optimal Power Flow - Base Case Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    # Parse bus data\n    buses = {}\n    for row in data['bus']:\n        bus_id = int(row[0])\n        buses[bus_id] = {\n            'id': bus_id,\n            'type': int(row[1]),  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref\n            'pd': row[2] / baseMVA,  # MW -> pu\n            'qd': row[3] / baseMVA,  # MVAr -> pu\n            'gs': row[4] / baseMVA,  # shunt conductance\n            'bs': row[5] / baseMVA,  # shunt susceptance\n            'vm': row[7],  # voltage magnitude\n            'va': row[8],  # voltage angle (degrees)\n            'vmin': row[11],\n            'vmax': row[12],\n        }\n\n    # Parse generator data\n    generators = []\n    for idx, row in enumerate(data['gen']):\n        bus_id = int(row[0])\n        gen = {\n            'id': idx,\n            'bus': bus_id,\n            'pg': row[1] / baseMVA,  # MW -> pu\n            'qg': row[2] / baseMVA,  # MVAr -> pu\n            'qmax': row[3] / baseMVA,\n            'qmin': row[4] / baseMVA,\n            'vg': row[5],\n            'mbase': row[6],\n            'status': int(row[7]),\n            'pmax': row[8] / baseMVA,\n            'pmin': row[9] / baseMVA,\n        }\n        if gen['status'] == 1:\n            generators.append(gen)\n\n    # Parse generator cost data\n    for idx, cost_row in enumerate(data['gencost']):\n        if idx < len(generators):\n            model = int(cost_row[0])  # 1=piecewise, 2=polynomial\n            ncost = int(cost_row[3])\n            if model == 2 and ncost == 3:\n                # Quadratic cost: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\n                generators[idx]['c2'] = cost_row[4] * baseMVA**2\n                generators[idx]['c1'] = cost_row[5] * baseMVA\n                generators[idx]['c0'] = cost_row[6]\n            else:\n                # Default to linear\n                generators[idx]['c2'] = 0.0\n                generators[idx]['c1'] = cost_row[-2] * baseMVA if len(cost_row) > 5 else 1000.0\n                generators[idx]['c0'] = cost_row[-1] if len(cost_row) > 6 else 0.0\n\n    # Parse branch data\n    branches = []\n    for row in data['branch']:\n        fbus = int(row[0])\n        tbus = int(row[1])\n\n        # Branch impedance and admittance\n        r = row[2]\n        x = row[3]\n        b = row[4]  # total line charging susceptance\n\n        if abs(r) < 1e-10 and abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            # Zero impedance - skip or handle specially\n            continue\n\n        # Series admittance\n        z = r + 1j*x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        # Shunt admittances (pi model)\n        yc_from = 1j * b / 2.0\n        yc_to = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Transformer tap ratio and phase shift\n        ratio = row[8] if abs(row[8]) > 1e-6 else 1.0\n        angle = row[9] * np.pi / 180.0  # degrees to radians\n        tap = ratio * np.exp(1j * angle)\n\n        # Rating\n        rateA = row[5] / baseMVA if row[5] > 0 else 9900.0\n\n        branch = {\n            'from': fbus,\n            'to': tbus,\n            'y': y,  # series admittance\n            'yc_from': yc_from,\n            'yc_to': yc_to,\n            'tap': tap,\n            'rateA': rateA,\n            'status': int(row[10]),\n        }\n\n        if branch['status'] == 1:\n            branches.append(branch)\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'generators': generators,\n        'branches': branches,\n    }\n\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve AC Optimal Power Flow using CasADi\"\"\"\n\n    buses = network['buses']\n    generators = network['generators']\n    branches = network['branches']\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    # Bus indices\n    bus_ids = sorted(buses.keys())\n    bus_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n    n_bus = len(bus_ids)\n    n_gen = len(generators)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    print(f\"Setting up ACOPF: {n_bus} buses, {n_gen} generators, {n_branch} branches\")\n\n    # Decision variables\n    # For each bus: Vr, Vi (real and imaginary voltage)\n    # For each gen: Pg, Qg\n    V_r = MX.sym('Vr', n_bus)\n    V_i = MX.sym('Vi', n_bus)\n    P_g = MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)\n    Q_g = MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)\n\n    # Stack all variables\n    x = vertcat(V_r, V_i, P_g, Q_g)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    obj = 0\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        c2 = gen.get('c2', 0.0)\n        c1 = gen.get('c1', 1000.0)\n        c0 = gen.get('c0', 0.0)\n        pg_mw = P_g[i] * baseMVA\n        obj += c2 * pg_mw**2 + c1 * pg_mw + c0\n\n    # Constraints\n    g = []  # equality constraints\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []  # inequality constraints\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Build generator mapping\n    gen_at_bus = {bid: [] for bid in bus_ids}\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        gen_at_bus[gen['bus']].append(i)\n\n    # Power balance equations (complex form)\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n\n        V_complex = V_r[i] + 1j * V_i[i]\n        V_mag_sq = V_r[i]**2 + V_i[i]**2\n\n        # Net generation at this bus\n        P_gen = sum(P_g[gi] for gi in gen_at_bus[bid]) if gen_at_bus[bid] else 0\n        Q_gen = sum(Q_g[gi] for gi in gen_at_bus[bid]) if gen_at_bus[bid] else 0\n\n        # Load at this bus\n        P_load = bus['pd']\n        Q_load = bus['qd']\n\n        # Shunt injection\n        P_shunt = bus['gs'] * V_mag_sq\n        Q_shunt = -bus['bs'] * V_mag_sq\n\n        # Branch flows from this bus\n        P_branch = 0\n        Q_branch = 0\n\n        for br in branches:\n            if br['from'] == bid:\n                # Forward flow\n                fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n                ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n                V_f = V_r[fi] + 1j * V_i[fi]\n                V_t = V_r[ti] + 1j * V_i[ti]\n\n                y = br['y']\n                yc_f = br['yc_from']\n                tap = br['tap']\n                tap_mag_sq = (tap.real**2 + tap.imag**2)\n\n                # S_ij = (y + yc_f)* |Vf|^2 / |tap|^2 - y* Vf Vt* / tap\n                # Using complex arithmetic in CasADi\n                V_f_r = V_r[fi]\n                V_f_i = V_i[fi]\n                V_t_r = V_r[ti]\n                V_t_i = V_i[ti]\n\n                # (y + yc_f)* |Vf|^2 / |tap|^2\n                y_tot = y + yc_f\n                y_tot_conj_r = y_tot.real\n                y_tot_conj_i = -y_tot.imag\n                V_mag_sq_f = V_f_r**2 + V_f_i**2\n                term1_r = y_tot_conj_r * V_mag_sq_f / tap_mag_sq\n                term1_i = y_tot_conj_i * V_mag_sq_f / tap_mag_sq\n\n                # -y* Vf Vt* / tap\n                y_conj_r = y.real\n                y_conj_i = -y.imag\n                tap_r = tap.real\n                tap_i = tap.imag\n\n                # Vf * Vt* = (Vf_r + j*Vf_i)(Vt_r - j*Vt_i)\n                VfVt_conj_r = V_f_r * V_t_r + V_f_i * V_t_i\n                VfVt_conj_i = V_f_i * V_t_r - V_f_r * V_t_i\n\n                # y* * (Vf*Vt*)\n                prod_r = y_conj_r * VfVt_conj_r - y_conj_i * VfVt_conj_i\n                prod_i = y_conj_r * VfVt_conj_i + y_conj_i * VfVt_conj_r\n\n                # / tap = / (tap_r + j*tap_i)\n                tap_mag_sq_br = tap_r**2 + tap_i**2\n                term2_r = (prod_r * tap_r + prod_i * tap_i) / tap_mag_sq_br\n                term2_i = (prod_i * tap_r - prod_r * tap_i) / tap_mag_sq_br\n\n                S_ij_r = term1_r - term2_r\n                S_ij_i = term1_i - term2_i\n\n                P_branch += S_ij_r\n                Q_branch += S_ij_i\n\n            if br['to'] == bid:\n                # Reverse flow\n                fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n                ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n                V_f = V_r[fi] + 1j * V_i[fi]\n                V_t = V_r[ti] + 1j * V_i[ti]\n\n                y = br['y']\n                yc_t = br['yc_to']\n                tap = br['tap']\n\n                V_f_r = V_r[fi]\n                V_f_i = V_i[fi]\n                V_t_r = V_r[ti]\n                V_t_i = V_i[ti]\n\n                # S_ji = (y + yc_t)* |Vt|^2 - y* Vf* Vt / tap*\n                y_tot = y + yc_t\n                y_tot_conj_r = y_tot.real\n                y_tot_conj_i = -y_tot.imag\n                V_mag_sq_t = V_t_r**2 + V_t_i**2\n                term1_r = y_tot_conj_r * V_mag_sq_t\n                term1_i = y_tot_conj_i * V_mag_sq_t\n\n                # y* Vf* Vt / tap*\n                y_conj_r = y.real\n                y_conj_i = -y.imag\n                tap_conj_r = tap.real\n                tap_conj_i = -tap.imag\n\n                # Vf* * Vt = (Vf_r - j*Vf_i)(Vt_r + j*Vt_i)\n                VfVt_r = V_f_r * V_t_r + V_f_i * V_t_i\n                VfVt_i = V_f_i * V_t_r - V_f_r * V_t_i\n                # Wait, let me recalculate\n                VfVt_i = -V_f_i * V_t_r + V_f_r * V_t_i\n\n                # Actually: Vf* Vt = (Vf_r - j Vf_i)(Vt_r + j Vt_i)\n                #                   = Vf_r*Vt_r + j*Vf_r*Vt_i - j*Vf_i*Vt_r + Vf_i*Vt_i\n                VfconjVt_r = V_f_r * V_t_r + V_f_i * V_t_i\n                VfconjVt_i = V_f_r * V_t_i - V_f_i * V_t_r\n\n                # y* * (Vf* Vt)\n                prod_r = y_conj_r * VfconjVt_r - y_conj_i * VfconjVt_i\n                prod_i = y_conj_r * VfconjVt_i + y_conj_i * VfconjVt_r\n\n                # / tap* = / (tap_conj_r + j*tap_conj_i)\n                tap_conj_mag_sq = tap_conj_r**2 + tap_conj_i**2\n                term2_r = (prod_r * tap_conj_r + prod_i * tap_conj_i) / tap_conj_mag_sq\n                term2_i = (prod_i * tap_conj_r - prod_r * tap_conj_i) / tap_conj_mag_sq\n\n                S_ji_r = term1_r - term2_r\n                S_ji_i = term1_i - term2_i\n\n                P_branch += S_ji_r\n                Q_branch += S_ji_i\n\n        # Power balance: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = P_branch\n        g.append(P_gen - P_load - P_shunt - P_branch)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n        # Q balance\n        g.append(Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt - Q_branch)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Reference bus angle constraint\n    ref_buses = [bid for bid in bus_ids if buses[bid]['type'] == 3]\n    if ref_buses:\n        ref_idx = bus_idx[ref_buses[0]]\n        g.append(V_i[ref_idx])  # Va = 0 means Vi = 0 (Vr = |V|)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Voltage magnitude limits\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        V_mag_sq = V_r[i]**2 + V_i[i]**2\n        h.append(V_mag_sq)\n        lbh.append(bus['vmin']**2)\n        ubh.append(bus['vmax']**2)\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    for br in branches:\n        fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n        ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n\n        # Forward flow S_ij\n        V_f_r = V_r[fi]\n        V_f_i = V_i[fi]\n        V_t_r = V_r[ti]\n        V_t_i = V_i[ti]\n\n        y = br['y']\n        yc_f = br['yc_from']\n        tap = br['tap']\n        tap_mag_sq = (tap.real**2 + tap.imag**2)\n\n        y_tot = y + yc_f\n        y_tot_conj_r = y_tot.real\n        y_tot_conj_i = -y_tot.imag\n        V_mag_sq_f = V_f_r**2 + V_f_i**2\n        term1_r = y_tot_conj_r * V_mag_sq_f / tap_mag_sq\n        term1_i = y_tot_conj_i * V_mag_sq_f / tap_mag_sq\n\n        y_conj_r = y.real\n        y_conj_i = -y.imag\n        tap_r = tap.real\n        tap_i = tap.imag\n\n        VfVt_conj_r = V_f_r * V_t_r + V_f_i * V_t_i\n        VfVt_conj_i = V_f_i * V_t_r - V_f_r * V_t_i\n\n        prod_r = y_conj_r * VfVt_conj_r - y_conj_i * VfVt_conj_i\n        prod_i = y_conj_r * VfVt_conj_i + y_conj_i * VfVt_conj_r\n\n        tap_mag_sq_br = tap_r**2 + tap_i**2\n        term2_r = (prod_r * tap_r + prod_i * tap_i) / tap_mag_sq_br\n        term2_i = (prod_i * tap_r - prod_r * tap_i) / tap_mag_sq_br\n\n        S_ij_r = term1_r - term2_r\n        S_ij_i = term1_i - term2_i\n\n        S_ij_mag_sq = S_ij_r**2 + S_ij_i**2\n        h.append(S_ij_mag_sq)\n        lbh.append(0)\n        ubh.append(br['rateA']**2)\n\n        # Reverse flow S_ji\n        y_tot = y + br['yc_to']\n        y_tot_conj_r = y_tot.real\n        y_tot_conj_i = -y_tot.imag\n        V_mag_sq_t = V_t_r**2 + V_t_i**2\n        term1_r = y_tot_conj_r * V_mag_sq_t\n        term1_i = y_tot_conj_i * V_mag_sq_t\n\n        VfconjVt_r = V_f_r * V_t_r + V_f_i * V_t_i\n        VfconjVt_i = V_f_r * V_t_i - V_f_i * V_t_r\n\n        prod_r = y_conj_r * VfconjVt_r - y_conj_i * VfconjVt_i\n        prod_i = y_conj_r * VfconjVt_i + y_conj_i * VfconjVt_r\n\n        tap_conj_r = tap.real\n        tap_conj_i = -tap.imag\n        tap_conj_mag_sq = tap_conj_r**2 + tap_conj_i**2\n        term2_r = (prod_r * tap_conj_r + prod_i * tap_conj_i) / tap_conj_mag_sq\n        term2_i = (prod_i * tap_conj_r - prod_r * tap_conj_i) / tap_conj_mag_sq\n\n        S_ji_r = term1_r - term2_r\n        S_ji_i = term1_i - term2_i\n\n        S_ji_mag_sq = S_ji_r**2 + S_ji_i**2\n        h.append(S_ji_mag_sq)\n        lbh.append(0)\n        ubh.append(br['rateA']**2)\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n\n    # Voltage real/imag bounds (approximate as [-vmax, vmax])\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        lbx.append(-bus['vmax'])\n        ubx.append(bus['vmax'])\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        lbx.append(-bus['vmax'])\n        ubx.append(bus['vmax'])\n\n    # Generator P bounds\n    for gen in generators:\n        lbx.append(gen['pmin'])\n        ubx.append(gen['pmax'])\n\n    # Generator Q bounds\n    for gen in generators:\n        lbx.append(gen['qmin'])\n        ubx.append(gen['qmax'])\n\n    # Initial guess\n    x0 = []\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        v0 = bus['vm']\n        a0 = bus['va'] * np.pi / 180.0\n        x0.append(v0 * np.cos(a0))  # Vr\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        v0 = bus['vm']\n        a0 = bus['va'] * np.pi / 180.0\n        x0.append(v0 * np.sin(a0))  # Vi\n    for gen in generators:\n        x0.append(gen['pg'])\n    for gen in generators:\n        x0.append(gen['qg'])\n\n    # Setup NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': obj,\n        'g': vertcat(*g, *h) if (g and h) else (vertcat(*g) if g else vertcat(*h))\n    }\n\n    lbg_all = lbg + lbh\n    ubg_all = ubg + ubh\n\n    # Solver options\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'print_time': 0,\n    }\n\n    solver = nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving ACOPF...\")\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg_all, ubg=ubg_all)\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n\n    V_r_opt = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n    V_i_opt = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    P_g_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Q_g_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    # Compute voltage magnitudes and angles\n    V_mag = np.sqrt(V_r_opt**2 + V_i_opt**2)\n    V_ang = np.arctan2(V_i_opt, V_r_opt) * 180 / np.pi\n\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    success = stats['success']\n\n    result = {\n        'success': success,\n        'solver_status': 'optimal' if success else 'infeasible',\n        'objective': float(sol['f']),\n        'bus_idx': bus_idx,\n        'V_mag': V_mag,\n        'V_ang': V_ang,\n        'P_g': P_g_opt,\n        'Q_g': Q_g_opt,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n    }\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef generate_report(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Generate the final report.json\"\"\"\n\n    buses = network['buses']\n    generators = network['generators']\n    branches = network['branches']\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    bus_idx = solution['bus_idx']\n    V_mag = solution['V_mag']\n    V_ang = solution['V_ang']\n    P_g = solution['P_g']\n    Q_g = solution['Q_g']\n\n    # Total generation\n    total_gen_mw = sum(P_g) * baseMVA\n    total_gen_mvar = sum(Q_g) * baseMVA\n\n    # Total load\n    total_load_mw = sum(b['pd'] for b in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n    total_load_mvar = sum(b['qd'] for b in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n\n    # Total losses\n    total_losses_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n    # Generator report\n    gen_report = []\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        gen_report.append({\n            'id': gen['id'],\n            'bus': gen['bus'],\n            'pg_MW': float(P_g[i] * baseMVA),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Q_g[i] * baseMVA),\n            'pmin_MW': float(gen['pmin'] * baseMVA),\n            'pmax_MW': float(gen['pmax'] * baseMVA),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(gen['qmin'] * baseMVA),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(gen['qmax'] * baseMVA),\n        })\n\n    # Bus report\n    bus_report = []\n    for bid in sorted(buses.keys()):\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        bus_report.append({\n            'id': bid,\n            'vm_pu': float(V_mag[i]),\n            'va_deg': float(V_ang[i]),\n            'vmin_pu': float(bus['vmin']),\n            'vmax_pu': float(bus['vmax']),\n        })\n\n    # Branch flows and loading\n    branch_loadings = []\n    for br in branches:\n        fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n        ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n\n        V_f = V_mag[fi] * np.exp(1j * V_ang[fi] * np.pi / 180)\n        V_t = V_mag[ti] * np.exp(1j * V_ang[ti] * np.pi / 180)\n\n        y = br['y']\n        yc_f = br['yc_from']\n        yc_t = br['yc_to']\n        tap = br['tap']\n\n        #
