Parse generator cost data\n    for idx, cost_row in enumerate(data['gencost']):\n        if idx < len(generators):\n            model = int(cost_row[0])\n            ncost = int(cost_row[3])\n            if model == 2 and ncost == 3:\n                generators[idx]['c2'] = cost_row[4] * baseMVA**2\n                generators[idx]['c1'] = cost_row[5] * baseMVA\n                generators[idx]['c0'] = cost_row[6]\n            else:\n                generators[idx]['c2'] = 0.0\n                generators[idx]['c1'] = cost_row[-2] * baseMVA if len(cost_row) > 5 else 1000.0\n                generators[idx]['c0'] = cost_row[-1] if len(cost_row) > 6 else 0.0\n\n    # Parse branch data\n    branches = []\n    for row in data['branch']:\n        fbus = int(row[0])\n        tbus = int(row[1])\n        r = row[2]\n        x = row[3]\n        b = row[4]\n\n        if abs(r) < 1e-10 and abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        z = r + 1j*x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n        yc_from = 1j * b / 2.0\n        yc_to = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        ratio = row[8] if abs(row[8]) > 1e-6 else 1.0\n        angle = row[9] * np.pi / 180.0\n        tap = ratio * np.exp(1j * angle)\n\n        rateA = row[5] / baseMVA if row[5] > 0 else 9900.0\n\n        branch = {\n            'from': fbus,\n            'to': tbus,\n            'y': y,\n            'yc_from': yc_from,\n            'yc_to': yc_to,\n            'tap': tap,\n            'rateA': rateA,\n            'status': int(row[10]),\n        }\n\n        if branch['status'] == 1:\n            branches.append(branch)\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'generators': generators,\n        'branches': branches,\n    }\n\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve ACOPF using polar formulation with CasADi\"\"\"\n\n    buses = network['buses']\n    generators = network['generators']\n    branches = network['branches']\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    bus_ids = sorted(buses.keys())\n    bus_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n    n_bus = len(bus_ids)\n    n_gen = len(generators)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    print(f\"Setting up ACOPF: {n_bus} buses, {n_gen} generators, {n_branch} branches\")\n\n    # Decision variables: voltage magnitude and angle at each bus\n    V_mag = MX.sym('V_mag', n_bus)\n    V_ang = MX.sym('V_ang', n_bus)\n    P_g = MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)\n    Q_g = MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)\n\n    x = vertcat(V_mag, V_ang, P_g, Q_g)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    obj = 0\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        c2 = gen.get('c2', 0.0)\n        c1 = gen.get('c1', 1000.0)\n        c0 = gen.get('c0', 0.0)\n        pg_mw = P_g[i] * baseMVA\n        obj += c2 * pg_mw**2 + c1 * pg_mw + c0\n\n    # Constraints\n    g = []\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Build generator mapping\n    gen_at_bus = {bid: [] for bid in bus_ids}\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        gen_at_bus[gen['bus']].append(i)\n\n    # Power balance equations using polar form\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n\n        vm_i = V_mag[i]\n        va_i = V_ang[i]\n\n        # Net generation at this bus\n        if gen_at_bus[bid]:\n            P_gen = sum(P_g[gi] for gi in gen_at_bus[bid])\n            Q_gen = sum(Q_g[gi] for gi in gen_at_bus[bid])\n        else:\n            P_gen = MX(0)\n            Q_gen = MX(0)\n\n        # Load\n        P_load = bus['pd']\n        Q_load = bus['qd']\n\n        # Shunt\n        P_shunt = bus['gs'] * vm_i**2\n        Q_shunt = -bus['bs'] * vm_i**2\n\n        # Branch flows: sum over all connected branches\n        P_branch = 0\n        Q_branch = 0\n\n        for br in branches:\n            if br['from'] == bid or br['to'] == bid:\n                fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n                ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n\n                vm_f = V_mag[fi]\n                va_f = V_ang[fi]\n                vm_t = V_mag[ti]\n                va_t = V_ang[ti]\n\n                y = br['y']\n                g_ser = y.real\n                b_ser = y.imag\n\n                tap = br['tap']\n                tap_mag = abs(tap)\n                tap_ang = np.angle(tap)\n\n                # Angle difference\n                theta_ij = va_f - va_t - tap_ang\n\n                if br['from'] == bid:\n                    # Forward flow from i to j\n                    # P_ij = (g/t^2) * vm_i^2 - (g*cos(theta) + b*sin(theta)) * vm_i * vm_j / t\n                    # Q_ij = -(b+bc)/t^2 * vm_i^2 + (b*cos(theta) - g*sin(theta)) * vm_i * vm_j / t\n\n                    yc_f = br['yc_from']\n                    b_shunt_f = yc_f.imag\n\n                    P_ij = (g_ser / tap_mag**2) * vm_f**2 - (g_ser * cos(theta_ij) + b_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n                    Q_ij = -(b_ser + b_shunt_f) / tap_mag**2 * vm_f**2 + (b_ser * cos(theta_ij) - g_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n                    P_branch += P_ij\n                    Q_branch += Q_ij\n\n                if br['to'] == bid:\n                    # Reverse flow from j to i\n                    yc_t = br['yc_to']\n                    b_shunt_t = yc_t.imag\n\n                    # Note: theta_ji = -theta_ij\n                    P_ji = g_ser * vm_t**2 - (g_ser * cos(theta_ij) - b_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n                    Q_ji = -(b_ser + b_shunt_t) * vm_t**2 - (b_ser * cos(theta_ij) + g_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n                    P_branch += P_ji\n                    Q_branch += Q_ji\n\n        # Power balance constraints\n        g.append(P_gen - P_load - P_shunt - P_branch)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n        g.append(Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt - Q_branch)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Reference bus angle\n    ref_buses = [bid for bid in bus_ids if buses[bid]['type'] == 3]\n    if ref_buses:\n        ref_idx = bus_idx[ref_buses[0]]\n        g.append(V_ang[ref_idx])\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    for br in branches:\n        fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n        ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n\n        vm_f = V_mag[fi]\n        va_f = V_ang[fi]\n        vm_t = V_mag[ti]\n        va_t = V_ang[ti]\n\n        y = br['y']\n        g_ser = y.real\n        b_ser = y.imag\n        tap = br['tap']\n        tap_mag = abs(tap)\n        tap_ang = np.angle(tap)\n        theta_ij = va_f - va_t - tap_ang\n\n        # Forward flow\n        yc_f = br['yc_from']\n        b_shunt_f = yc_f.imag\n\n        P_ij = (g_ser / tap_mag**2) * vm_f**2 - (g_ser * cos(theta_ij) + b_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n        Q_ij = -(b_ser + b_shunt_f) / tap_mag**2 * vm_f**2 + (b_ser * cos(theta_ij) - g_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n        S_ij_sq = P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2\n        h.append(S_ij_sq)\n        lbh.append(0)\n        ubh.append(br['rateA']**2)\n\n        # Reverse flow\n        yc_t = br['yc_to']\n        b_shunt_t = yc_t.imag\n\n        P_ji = g_ser * vm_t**2 - (g_ser * cos(theta_ij) - b_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n        Q_ji = -(b_ser + b_shunt_t) * vm_t**2 - (b_ser * cos(theta_ij) + g_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n        S_ji_sq = P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2\n        h.append(S_ji_sq)\n        lbh.append(0)\n        ubh.append(br['rateA']**2)\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        lbx.append(bus['vmin'])\n        ubx.append(bus['vmax'])\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        lbx.append(-np.pi)\n        ubx.append(np.pi)\n\n    # Generator P bounds\n    for gen in generators:\n        lbx.append(gen['pmin'])\n        ubx.append(gen['pmax'])\n\n    # Generator Q bounds\n    for gen in generators:\n        lbx.append(gen['qmin'])\n        ubx.append(gen['qmax'])\n\n    # Initial guess\n    x0 = []\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        x0.append(buses[bid]['vm'])\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        x0.append(buses[bid]['va'] * np.pi / 180.0)\n    for gen in generators:\n        x0.append(gen['pg'])\n    for gen in generators:\n        x0.append(gen['qg'])\n\n    # Setup NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': obj,\n        'g': vertcat(*g, *h) if (g and h) else (vertcat(*g) if g else vertcat(*h))\n    }\n\n    lbg_all = lbg + lbh\n    ubg_all = ubg + ubh\n\n    # Solver options\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'print_time': 0,\n    }\n\n    solver = nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving ACOPF...\")\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg_all, ubg=ubg_all)\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n\n    V_mag_opt = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n    V_ang_opt = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus] * 180 / np.pi  # Convert to degrees\n    P_g_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Q_g_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    success = stats['success']\n\n    result = {\n        'success': success,\n        'solver_status': 'optimal' if success else 'infeasible',\n        'objective': float(sol['f']),\n        'bus_idx': bus_idx,\n        'V_mag': V_mag_opt,\n        'V_ang': V_ang_opt,\n        'P_g': P_g_opt,\n        'Q_g': Q_g_opt,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n    }\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef generate_report(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Generate the final report.json\"\"\"\n\n    buses = network['buses']\n    generators = network['generators']\n    branches = network['branches']\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    bus_idx = solution['bus_idx']\n    V_mag = solution['V_mag']\n    V_ang = solution['V_ang']\n    P_g = solution['P_g']\n    Q_g = solution['Q_g']\n\n    # Total generation\n    total_gen_mw = sum(P_g) * baseMVA\n    total_gen_mvar = sum(Q_g) * baseMVA\n\n    # Total load\n    total_load_mw = sum(b['pd'] for b in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n    total_load_mvar = sum(b['qd'] for b in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n\n    # Total losses\n    total_losses_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n    # Generator report\n    gen_report = []\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        gen_report.append({\n            'id': gen['id'],\n            'bus': gen['bus'],\n            'pg_MW': float(P_g[i] * baseMVA),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Q_g[i] * baseMVA),\n            'pmin_MW': float(gen['pmin'] * baseMVA),\n            'pmax_MW': float(gen['pmax'] * baseMVA),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(gen['qmin'] * baseMVA),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(gen['qmax'] * baseMVA),\n        })\n\n    # Bus report\n    bus_report = []\n    for bid in sorted(buses.keys()):\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        bus_report.append({\n            'id': bid,\n            'vm_pu': float(V_mag[i]),\n            'va_deg': float(V_ang[i]),\n            'vmin_pu': float(bus['vmin']),\n            'vmax_pu': float(bus['vmax']),\n        })\n\n    # Branch flows and loading\n    branch_loadings = []\n    for br in branches:\n        fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n        ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n\n        vm_f = V_mag[fi]\n        va_f = V_ang[fi] * np.pi / 180\n        vm_t = V_mag[ti]\n        va_t = V_ang[ti] * np.pi / 180\n\n        y = br['y']\n        g_ser = y.real\n        b_ser = y.imag\n        tap = br['tap']\n        tap_mag = abs(tap)\n        tap_ang = np.angle(tap)\n        theta_ij = va_f - va_t - tap_ang\n\n        # Forward flow\n        yc_f = br['yc_from']\n        b_shunt_f = yc_f.imag\n\n        P_ij = (g_ser / tap_mag**2) * vm_f**2 - (g_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) + b_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n        Q_ij = -(b_ser + b_shunt_f) / tap_mag**2 * vm_f**2 + (b_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) - g_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n        flow_from_mva = np.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2) * baseMVA\n\n        # Reverse flow\n        yc_t = br['yc_to']\n        b_shunt_t = yc_t.imag\n\n        P_ji = g_ser * vm_t**2 - (g_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) - b_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n        Q_ji = -(b_ser + b_shunt_t) * vm_t**2 - (b_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) + g_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n        flow_to_mva = np.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2) * baseMVA\n\n        limit_mva = br['rateA'] * baseMVA\n        loading_pct = max(flow_from_mva, flow_to_mva) / limit_mva * 100 if limit_mva > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_loadings.append({\n            'from_bus': br['from'],\n            'to_bus': br['to'],\n            'loading_pct': float(loading_pct),\n            'flow_from_MVA': float(flow_from_mva),\n            'flow_to_MVA': float(flow_to_mva),\n            'limit_MVA': float(limit_mva),\n        })\n\n    # Most loaded branches\n    branch_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_loadings[:10]\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    max_p_mismatch = 0.0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0.0\n\n    gen_at_bus = {}\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        if gen['bus'] not in gen_at_bus:\n            gen_at_bus[gen['bus']] = []\n        gen_at_bus[gen['bus']].append(i)\n\n    for bid in buses.keys():\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n\n        vm_i = V_mag[i]\n        va_i = V_ang[i] * np.pi / 180\n\n        # Generation\n        P_gen = sum(P_g[gi] for gi in gen_at_bus.get(bid, []))\n        Q_gen = sum(Q_g[gi] for gi in gen_at_bus.get(bid, []))\n\n        # Load and shunt\n        P_load = bus['pd']\n        Q_load = bus['qd']\n        P_shunt = bus['gs'] * vm_i**2\n        Q_shunt = -bus['bs'] * vm_i**2\n\n        # Branch flows\n        P_branch = 0\n        Q_branch = 0\n\n        for br in branches:\n            if br['from'] == bid or br['to'] == bid:\n                fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n                ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n\n                vm_f = V_mag[fi]\n                va_f = V_ang[fi] * np.pi / 180\n                vm_t = V_mag[ti]\n                va_t = V_ang[ti] * np.pi / 180\n\n                y = br['y']\n                g_ser = y.real\n                b_ser = y.imag\n                tap = br['tap']\n                tap_mag = abs(tap)\n                tap_ang = np.angle(tap)\n                theta_ij = va_f - va_t - tap_ang\n\n                if br['from'] == bid:\n                    yc_f = br['yc_from']\n                    b_shunt_f = yc_f.imag\n\n                    P_ij = (g_ser / tap_mag**2) * vm_f**2 - (g_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) + b_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n                    Q_ij = -(b_ser + b_shunt_f) / tap_mag**2 * vm_f**2 + (b_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) - g_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n                    P_branch += P_ij\n                    Q_branch += Q_ij\n\n                if br['to'] == bid:\n                    yc_t = br['yc_to']\n                    b_shunt_t = yc_t.imag\n\n                    P_ji = g_ser * vm_t**2 - (g_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) - b_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n                    Q_ji = -(b_ser + b_shunt_t) * vm_t**2 - (b_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) + g_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n                    P_branch += P_ji\n                    Q_branch += Q_ji\n\n        p_mismatch = abs(P_gen - P_load - P_shunt - P_branch)\n        q_mismatch = abs(Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt - Q_branch)\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n    # Parse bus data\n    buses = {}\n    for row in data['bus']:\n        bus_id = int(row[0])\n        buses[bus_id] = {\n            'id': bus_id,\n            'type': int(row[1]),\n            'pd': row[2] / baseMVA,\n            'qd': row[3] / baseMVA,\n            'gs': row[4] / baseMVA,\n            'bs': row[5] / baseMVA,\n            'vm': row[7],\n            'va': row[8],\n            'vmin': row[11],\n            'vmax': row[12],\n        }\n\n    # Parse branch data\n    branches = []\n    for row in data['branch']:\n        fbus = int(row[0])\n        tbus = int(row[1])\n        r = row[2]\n        x = row[3]\n        b = row[4]\n\n        if abs(r) < 1e-10 and abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        z = r + 1j*x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n        yc_from = 1j * b / 2.0\n        yc_to = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        ratio = row[8] if abs(row[8]) > 1e-6 else 1.0\n        angle = row[9] * np.pi / 180.0\n        tap = ratio * np.exp(1j * angle)\n\n        rateA = row[5] / baseMVA if row[5] > 0 else 9900.0\n\n        branch = {\n            'from': fbus,\n            'to': tbus,\n            'y': y,\n            'yc_from': yc_from,\n            'yc_to': yc_to,\n            'tap': tap,\n            'rateA': rateA,\n            'status': int(row[10]),\n        }\n\n        if branch['status'] == 1:\n            branches.append(branch)\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'generators': generators,\n        'branches': branches,\n    }\n\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve ACOPF using polar formulation with CasADi\"\"\"\n\n    buses = network['buses']\n    generators = network['generators']\n    branches = network['branches']\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    bus_ids = sorted(buses.keys())\n    bus_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n    n_bus = len(bus_ids)\n    n_gen = len(generators)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    print(f\"Setting up ACOPF: {n_bus} buses, {n_gen} generators, {n_branch} branches\")\n\n    # Decision variables: voltage magnitude and angle at each bus\n    V_mag = MX.sym('V_mag', n_bus)\n    V_ang = MX.sym('V_ang', n_bus)\n    P_g = MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)\n    Q_g = MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)\n\n    x = vertcat(V_mag, V_ang, P_g, Q_g)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    obj = 0\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        c2 = gen.get('c2', 0.0)\n        c1 = gen.get('c1', 1000.0)\n        c0 = gen.get('c0', 0.0)\n        pg_mw = P_g[i] * baseMVA\n        obj += c2 * pg_mw**2 + c1 * pg_mw + c0\n\n    # Constraints\n    g = []\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Build generator mapping\n    gen_at_bus = {bid: [] for bid in bus_ids}\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        gen_at_bus[gen['bus']].append(i)\n\n    # Power balance equations using polar form\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n\n        vm_i = V_mag[i]\n        va_i = V_ang[i]\n\n        # Net generation at this bus\n        if gen_at_bus[bid]:\n            P_gen = sum(P_g[gi] for gi in gen_at_bus[bid])\n            Q_gen = sum(Q_g[gi] for gi in gen_at_bus[bid])\n        else:\n            P_gen = MX(0)\n            Q_gen = MX(0)\n\n        # Load\n        P_load = bus['pd']\n        Q_load = bus['qd']\n\n        # Shunt\n        P_shunt = bus['gs'] * vm_i**2\n        Q_shunt = -bus['bs'] * vm_i**2\n\n        # Branch flows: sum over all connected branches\n        P_branch = MX(0)\n        Q_branch = MX(0)\n\n        for br in branches:\n            if br['from'] == bid or br['to'] == bid:\n                fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n                ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n\n                vm_f = V_mag[fi]\n                va_f = V_ang[fi]\n                vm_t = V_mag[ti]\n                va_t = V_ang[ti]\n\n                y = br['y']\n                g_ser = y.real\n                b_ser = y.imag\n\n                tap = br['tap']\n                tap_mag = abs(tap)\n                tap_ang = np.angle(tap)\n\n                # Angle difference\n                theta_ij = va_f - va_t - tap_ang\n\n                if br['from'] == bid:\n                    # Forward flow from i to j\n                    # P_ij = (g/t^2) * vm_i^2 - (g*cos(theta) + b*sin(theta)) * vm_i * vm_j / t\n                    # Q_ij = -(b+bc)/t^2 * vm_i^2 + (b*cos(theta) - g*sin(theta)) * vm_i * vm_j / t\n\n                    yc_f = br['yc_from']\n                    b_shunt_f = yc_f.imag\n\n                    P_ij = (g_ser / tap_mag**2) * vm_f**2 - (g_ser * cos(theta_ij) + b_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n                    Q_ij = -(b_ser + b_shunt_f) / tap_mag**2 * vm_f**2 + (b_ser * cos(theta_ij) - g_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n                    P_branch += P_ij\n                    Q_branch += Q_ij\n\n                if br['to'] == bid:\n                    # Reverse flow from j to i\n                    yc_t = br['yc_to']\n                    b_shunt_t = yc_t.imag\n\n                    # Note: theta_ji = -theta_ij\n                    P_ji = g_ser * vm_t**2 - (g_ser * cos(theta_ij) - b_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n                    Q_ji = -(b_ser + b_shunt_t) * vm_t**2 - (b_ser * cos(theta_ij) + g_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n                    P_branch += P_ji\n                    Q_branch += Q_ji\n\n        # Power balance constraints\n        g.append(P_gen - P_load - P_shunt - P_branch)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n        g.append(Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt - Q_branch)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Reference bus angle\n    ref_buses = [bid for bid in bus_ids if buses[bid]['type'] == 3]\n    if ref_buses:\n        ref_idx = bus_idx[ref_buses[0]]\n        g.append(V_ang[ref_idx])\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    for br in branches:\n        fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n        ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n\n        vm_f = V_mag[fi]\n        va_f = V_ang[fi]\n        vm_t = V_mag[ti]\n        va_t = V_ang[ti]\n\n        y = br['y']\n        g_ser = y.real\n        b_ser = y.imag\n        tap = br['tap']\n        tap_mag = abs(tap)\n        tap_ang = np.angle(tap)\n        theta_ij = va_f - va_t - tap_ang\n\n        # Forward flow\n        yc_f = br['yc_from']\n        b_shunt_f = yc_f.imag\n\n        P_ij = (g_ser / tap_mag**2) * vm_f**2 - (g_ser * cos(theta_ij) + b_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n        Q_ij = -(b_ser + b_shunt_f) / tap_mag**2 * vm_f**2 + (b_ser * cos(theta_ij) - g_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n        S_ij_sq = P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2\n        h.append(S_ij_sq)\n        lbh.append(0)\n        ubh.append(br['rateA']**2)\n\n        # Reverse flow\n        yc_t = br['yc_to']\n        b_shunt_t = yc_t.imag\n\n        P_ji = g_ser * vm_t**2 - (g_ser * cos(theta_ij) - b_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n        Q_ji = -(b_ser + b_shunt_t) * vm_t**2 - (b_ser * cos(theta_ij) + g_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n        S_ji_sq = P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2\n        h.append(S_ji_sq)\n        lbh.append(0)\n        ubh.append(br['rateA']**2)\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        lbx.append(bus['vmin'])\n        ubx.append(bus['vmax'])\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        lbx.append(-np.pi)\n        ubx.append(np.pi)\n\n    # Generator P bounds\n    for gen in generators:\n        lbx.append(gen['pmin'])\n        ubx.append(gen['pmax'])\n\n    # Generator Q bounds\n    for gen in generators:\n        lbx.append(gen['qmin'])\n        ubx.append(gen['qmax'])\n\n    # Initial guess\n    x0 = []\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        x0.append(buses[bid]['vm'])\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        x0.append(buses[bid]['va'] * np.pi / 180.0)\n    for gen in generators:\n        x0.append(gen['pg'])\n    for gen in generators:\n        x0.append(gen['qg'])\n\n    # Setup NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': obj,\n        'g': vertcat(*g, *h) if (g and h) else (vertcat(*g) if g else vertcat(*h))\n    }\n\n    lbg_all = lbg + lbh\n    ubg_all = ubg + ubh\n\n    # Solver options\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'print_time': 0,\n    }\n\n    solver = nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving ACOPF...\")\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg_all, ubg=ubg_all)\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n\n    V_mag_opt = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n    V_ang_opt = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus] * 180 / np.pi  # Convert to degrees\n    P_g_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Q_g_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    success = stats['success']\n\n    result = {\n        'success': success,\n        'solver_status': 'optimal' if success else 'infeasible',\n        'objective': float(sol['f']),\n        'bus_idx': bus_idx,\n        'V_mag': V_mag_opt,\n        'V_ang': V_ang_opt,\n        'P_g': P_g_opt,\n        'Q_g': Q_g_opt,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n    }\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef generate_report(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Generate the final report.json\"\"\"\n\n    buses = network['buses']\n    generators = network['generators']\n    branches = network['branches']\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    bus_idx = solution['bus_idx']\n    V_mag = solution['V_mag']\n    V_ang = solution['V_ang']\n    P_g = solution['P_g']\n    Q_g = solution['Q_g']\n\n    # Total generation\n    total_gen_mw = sum(P_g) * baseMVA\n    total_gen_mvar = sum(Q_g) * baseMVA\n\n    # Total load\n    total_load_mw = sum(b['pd'] for b in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n    total_load_mvar = sum(b['qd'] for b in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n\n    # Total losses\n    total_losses_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n    # Generator report\n    gen_report = []\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        gen_report.append({\n            'id': gen['id'],\n            'bus': gen['bus'],\n            'pg_MW': float(P_g[i] * baseMVA),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Q_g[i] * baseMVA),\n            'pmin_MW': float(gen['pmin'] * baseMVA),\n            'pmax_MW': float(gen['pmax'] * baseMVA),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(gen['qmin'] * baseMVA),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(gen['qmax'] * baseMVA),\n        })\n\n    # Bus report\n    bus_report = []\n    for bid in sorted(buses.keys()):\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        bus_report.append({\n            'id': bid,\n            'vm_pu': float(V_mag[i]),\n            'va_deg': float(V_ang[i]),\n            'vmin_pu': float(bus['vmin']),\n            'vmax_pu': float(bus['vmax']),\n        })\n\n    # Branch flows and loading\n    branch_loadings = []\n    for br in branches:\n        fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n        ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n\n        vm_f = V_mag[fi]\n        va_f = V_ang[fi] * np.pi / 180\n        vm_t = V_mag[ti]\n        va_t = V_ang[ti] * np.pi / 180\n\n        y = br['y']\n        g_ser = y.real\n        b_ser = y.imag\n        tap = br['tap']\n        tap_mag = abs(tap)\n        tap_ang = np.angle(tap)\n        theta_ij = va_f - va_t - tap_ang\n\n        # Forward flow\n        yc_f = br['yc_from']\n        b_shunt_f = yc_f.imag\n\n        P_ij = (g_ser / tap_mag**2) * vm_f**2 - (g_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) + b_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n        Q_ij = -(b_ser + b_shunt_f) / tap_mag**2 * vm_f**2 + (b_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) - g_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n        flow_from_mva = np.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2) * baseMVA\n\n        # Reverse flow\n        yc_t = br['yc_to']\n        b_shunt_t = yc_t.imag\n\n        P_ji = g_ser * vm_t**2 - (g_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) - b_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n        Q_ji = -(b_ser + b_shunt_t) * vm_t**2 - (b_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) + g_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n        flow_to_mva = np.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2) * baseMVA\n\n        limit_mva = br['rateA'] * baseMVA\n        loading_pct = max(flow_from_mva, flow_to_mva) / limit_mva * 100 if limit_mva > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_loadings.append({\n            'from_bus': br['from'],\n            'to_bus': br['to'],\n            'loading_pct': float(loading_pct),\n            'flow_from_MVA': float(flow_from_mva),\n            'flow_to_MVA': float(flow_to_mva),\n            'limit_MVA': float(limit_mva),\n        })\n\n    # Most loaded branches\n    branch_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_loadings[:10]\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    max_p_mismatch = 0.0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0.0\n\n    gen_at_bus = {}\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        if gen['bus'] not in gen_at_bus:\n            gen_at_bus[gen['bus']] = []\n        gen_at_bus[gen['bus']].append(i)\n\n    for bid in buses.keys():\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n\n        vm_i = V_mag[i]\n        va_i = V_ang[i] * np.pi / 180\n\n        # Generation\n        P_gen = sum(P_g[gi] for gi in gen_at_bus.get(bid, []))\n        Q_gen = sum(Q_g[gi] for gi in gen_at_bus.get(bid, []))\n\n        # Load and shunt\n        P_load = bus['pd']\n        Q_load = bus['qd']\n        P_shunt = bus['gs'] * vm_i**2\n        Q_shunt = -bus['bs'] * vm_i**2\n\n        # Branch flows\n        P_branch = 0\n        Q_branch = 0\n\n        for br in branches:\n            if br['from'] == bid or br['to'] == bid:\n                fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n                ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n\n                vm_f = V_mag[fi]\n                va_f = V_ang[fi] * np.pi / 180\n                vm_t = V_mag[ti]\n                va_t = V_ang[ti] * np.pi / 180\n\n                y = br['y']\n                g_ser = y.real\n                b_ser = y.imag\n                tap = br['tap']\n                tap_mag = abs(tap)\n                tap_ang = np.angle(tap)\n                theta_ij = va_f - va_t - tap_ang\n\n                if br['from'] == bid:\n                    yc_f = br['yc_from']\n                    b_shunt_f = yc_f.imag\n\n                    P_ij = (g_ser / tap_mag**2) * vm_f**2 - (g_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) + b_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n                    Q_ij = -(b_ser + b_shunt_f) / tap_mag**2 * vm_f**2 + (b_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) - g_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n                    P_branch += P_ij\n                    Q_branch += Q_ij\n\n                if br['to'] == bid:\n                    yc_t = br['yc_to']\n                    b_shunt_t = yc_t.imag\n\n                    P_ji = g_ser * vm_t**2 - (g_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) - b_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n                    Q_ji = -(b_ser + b_shunt_t) * vm_t**2 - (b_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) + g_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n                    P_branch += P_ji\n                    Q_branch += Q_ji\n\n        p_mismatch = abs(P_gen - P_load - P_shunt - P_branch)\n        q_mismatch = abs(Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt - Q_branch)\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n    # Branch overloads\n    max_overload = 0.0\n    for bl in branch_loadings:\n        overload = max(bl['flow_from_MVA'], bl['flow_to_MVA']) - bl['limit_MVA']\n        if overload > 0:\n            max_overload = max(max_overload, overload)\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(solution['objective']),\n            'total_load_MW': float(total_load_mw),\n            'total_load_MVAr': float(total_load_mvar),\n            'total_generation_MW': float(total_gen_mw),\n            'total_generation_MVAr': float(total_gen_mvar),\n            'total_losses_MW': float(total_losses_mw),\n            'solver_status': solution['solver_status'],\n        },\n        'generators': gen_report,\n        'buses': bus_report,\n        'most_loaded_branches': most_loaded,\n        'feasibility_check': {\n            'max_p_mismatch_MW': float(max_p_mismatch * baseMVA),\n            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': float(max_q_mismatch * baseMVA),\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_v_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_overload),\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(\"AC Optimal Power Flow - Base Case Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    # Parse bus data\n    buses = {}\n    for row in data['bus']:\n        bus_id = int(row[0])\n        buses[bus_id] = {\n            'id': bus_id,\n            'type': int(row[1]),\n            'pd': row[2] / baseMVA,\n            'qd': row[3] / baseMVA,\n            'gs': row[4] / baseMVA,\n            'bs': row[5] / baseMVA,\n            'vm': row[7],\n            'va': row[8],\n            'vmin': row[11],\n            'vmax': row[12],\n        }\n\n    # Parse generator data\n    generators = []\n    for idx, row in enumerate(data['gen']):\n        bus_id = int(row[0])\n        gen = {\n            'id': idx,\n            'bus': bus_id,\n            'pg': row[1] / baseMVA,\n            'qg': row[2] / baseMVA,\n            'qmax': row[3] / baseMVA,\n            'qmin': row[4] / baseMVA,\n            'vg': row[5],\n            'mbase': row[6],\n            'status': int(row[7]),\n            'pmax': row[8] / baseMVA,\n            'pmin': row[9] / baseMVA,\n        }\n        if gen['status'] == 1:\n            generators.append(gen)\n\n    # Parse generator cost data\n    for idx, cost_row in enumerate(data['gencost']):\n        if idx < len(generators):\n            model = int(cost_row[0])\n            ncost = int(cost_row[3])\n            if model == 2 and ncost == 3:\n                generators[idx]['c2'] = cost_row[4] * baseMVA**2\n                generators[idx]['c1'] = cost_row[5] * baseMVA\n                generators[idx]['c0'] = cost_row[6]\n            else:\n                generators[idx]['c2'] = 0.0\n                generators[idx]['c1'] = cost_row[-2] * baseMVA if len(cost_row) > 5 else 1000.0\n                generators[idx]['c0'] = cost_row[-1] if len(cost_row) > 6 else 0.0\n\n    # Parse branch data\n    branches = []\n    for row in data['branch']:\n        fbus = int(row[0])\n        tbus = int(row[1])\n        r = row[2]\n        x = row[3]\n        b = row[4]\n\n        if abs(r) < 1e-10 and abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        z = r + 1j*x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n        yc_from = 1j * b / 2.0\n        yc_to = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        ratio = row[8] if abs(row[8]) > 1e-6 else 1.0\n        angle = row[9] * np.pi / 180.0\n        tap = ratio * np.exp(1j * angle)\n\n        rateA = row[5] / baseMVA if row[5] > 0 else 9900.0\n\n        branch = {\n            'from': fbus,\n            'to': tbus,\n            'y': y,\n            'yc_from': yc_from,\n            'yc_to': yc_to,\n            'tap': tap,\n            'rateA': rateA,\n            'status': int(row[10]),\n        }\n\n        if branch['status'] == 1:\n            branches.append(branch)\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'generators': generators,\n        'branches': branches,\n    }\n\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve ACOPF using polar formulation with CasADi\"\"\"\n\n    buses = network['buses']\n    generators = network['generators']\n    branches = network['branches']\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    bus_ids = sorted(buses.keys())\n    bus_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n    n_bus = len(bus_ids)\n    n_gen = len(generators)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    print(f\"Setting up ACOPF: {n_bus} buses, {n_gen} generators, {n_branch} branches\")\n\n    # Decision variables: voltage magnitude and angle at each bus\n    V_mag = MX.sym('V_mag', n_bus)\n    V_ang = MX.sym('V_ang', n_bus)\n    P_g = MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)\n    Q_g = MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)\n\n    x = vertcat(V_mag, V_ang, P_g, Q_g)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    obj = 0\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        c2 = gen.get('c2', 0.0)\n        c1 = gen.get('c1', 1000.0)\n        c0 = gen.get('c0', 0.0)\n        pg_mw = P_g[i] * baseMVA\n        obj += c2 * pg_mw**2 + c1 * pg_mw + c0\n\n    # Constraints\n    g = []\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Build generator mapping\n    gen_at_bus = {bid: [] for bid in bus_ids}\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        gen_at_bus[gen['bus']].append(i)\n\n    # Power balance equations using polar form\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n\n        vm_i = V_mag[i]\n        va_i = V_ang[i]\n\n        # Net generation at this bus\n        P_gen = MX(0)\n        Q_gen = MX(0)\n        for gi in gen_at_bus[bid]:\n            P_gen = P_gen + P_g[gi]\n            Q_gen = Q_gen + Q_g[gi]\n\n        # Load (convert to MX)\n        P_load = MX(bus['pd'])\n        Q_load = MX(bus['qd'])\n\n        # Shunt\n        P_shunt = MX(bus['gs']) * vm_i**2\n        Q_shunt = -MX(bus['bs']) * vm_i**2\n\n        # Branch flows: sum over all connected branches\n        P_branch = MX(0)\n        Q_branch = MX(0)\n\n        for br in branches:\n            if br['from'] == bid or br['to'] == bid:\n                fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n                ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n\n                vm_f = V_mag[fi]\n                va_f = V_ang[fi]\n                vm_t = V_mag[ti]\n                va_t = V_ang[ti]\n\n                y = br['y']\n                g_ser = y.real\n                b_ser = y.imag\n\n                tap = br['tap']\n                tap_mag = abs(tap)\n                tap_ang = np.angle(tap)\n\n                # Angle difference\n                theta_ij = va_f - va_t - tap_ang\n\n                if br['from'] == bid:\n                    # Forward flow from i to j\n                    # P_ij = (g/t^2) * vm_i^2 - (g*cos(theta) + b*sin(theta)) * vm_i * vm_j / t\n                    # Q_ij = -(b+bc)/t^2 * vm_i^2 + (b*cos(theta) - g*sin(theta)) * vm_i * vm_j / t\n\n                    yc_f = br['yc_from']\n                    b_shunt_f = yc_f.imag\n\n                    P_ij = (g_ser / tap_mag**2) * vm_f**2 - (g_ser * cos(theta_ij) + b_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n                    Q_ij = -(b_ser + b_shunt_f) / tap_mag**2 * vm_f**2 + (b_ser * cos(theta_ij) - g_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n                    P_branch += P_ij\n                    Q_branch += Q_ij\n\n                if br['to'] == bid:\n                    # Reverse flow from j to i\n                    yc_t = br['yc_to']\n                    b_shunt_t = yc_t.imag\n\n                    # Note: theta_ji = -theta_ij\n                    P_ji = g_ser * vm_t**2 - (g_ser * cos(theta_ij) - b_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n                    Q_ji = -(b_ser + b_shunt_t) * vm_t**2 - (b_ser * cos(theta_ij) + g_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n                    P_branch += P_ji\n                    Q_branch += Q_ji\n\n        # Power balance constraints\n        g.append(P_gen - P_load - P_shunt - P_branch)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n        g.append(Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt - Q_branch)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Reference bus angle\n    ref_buses = [bid for bid in bus_ids if buses[bid]['type'] == 3]\n    if ref_buses:\n        ref_idx = bus_idx[ref_buses[0]]\n        g.append(V_ang[ref_idx])\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    for br in branches:\n        fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n        ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n\n        vm_f = V_mag[fi]\n        va_f = V_ang[fi]\n        vm_t = V_mag[ti]\n        va_t = V_ang[ti]\n\n        y = br['y']\n        g_ser = y.real\n        b_ser = y.imag\n        tap = br['tap']\n        tap_mag = abs(tap)\n        tap_ang = np.angle(tap)\n        theta_ij = va_f - va_t - tap_ang\n\n        # Forward flow\n        yc_f = br['yc_from']\n        b_shunt_f = yc_f.imag\n\n        P_ij = (g_ser / tap_mag**2) * vm_f**2 - (g_ser * cos(theta_ij) + b_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n        Q_ij = -(b_ser + b_shunt_f) / tap_mag**2 * vm_f**2 + (b_ser * cos(theta_ij) - g_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n        S_ij_sq = P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2\n        h.append(S_ij_sq)\n        lbh.append(0)\n        ubh.append(br['rateA']**2)\n\n        # Reverse flow\n        yc_t = br['yc_to']\n        b_shunt_t = yc_t.imag\n\n        P_ji = g_ser * vm_t**2 - (g_ser * cos(theta_ij) - b_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n        Q_ji = -(b_ser + b_shunt_t) * vm_t**2 - (b_ser * cos(theta_ij) + g_ser * sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n        S_ji_sq = P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2\n        h.append(S_ji_sq)\n        lbh.append(0)\n        ubh.append(br['rateA']**2)\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        lbx.append(bus['vmin'])\n        ubx.append(bus['vmax'])\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        lbx.append(-np.pi)\n        ubx.append(np.pi)\n\n    # Generator P bounds\n    for gen in generators:\n        lbx.append(gen['pmin'])\n        ubx.append(gen['pmax'])\n\n    # Generator Q bounds\n    for gen in generators:\n        lbx.append(gen['qmin'])\n        ubx.append(gen['qmax'])\n\n    # Initial guess\n    x0 = []\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        x0.append(buses[bid]['vm'])\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        x0.append(buses[bid]['va'] * np.pi / 180.0)\n    for gen in generators:\n        x0.append(gen['pg'])\n    for gen in generators:\n        x0.append(gen['qg'])\n\n    # Setup NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': obj,\n        'g': vertcat(*g, *h) if (g and h) else (vertcat(*g) if g else vertcat(*h))\n    }\n\n    lbg_all = lbg + lbh\n    ubg_all = ubg + ubh\n\n    # Solver options\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'print_time': 0,\n    }\n\n    solver = nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving ACOPF...\")\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg_all, ubg=ubg_all)\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n\n    V_mag_opt = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n    V_ang_opt = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus] * 180 / np.pi  # Convert to degrees\n    P_g_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Q_g_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    success = stats['success']\n\n    result = {\n        'success': success,\n        'solver_status': 'optimal' if success else 'infeasible',\n        'objective': float(sol['f']),\n        'bus_idx': bus_idx,\n        'V_mag': V_mag_opt,\n        'V_ang': V_ang_opt,\n        'P_g': P_g_opt,\n        'Q_g': Q_g_opt,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n    }\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef generate_report(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Generate the final report.json\"\"\"\n\n    buses = network['buses']\n    generators = network['generators']\n    branches = network['branches']\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    bus_idx = solution['bus_idx']\n    V_mag = solution['V_mag']\n    V_ang = solution['V_ang']\n    P_g = solution['P_g']\n    Q_g = solution['Q_g']\n\n    # Total generation\n    total_gen_mw = sum(P_g) * baseMVA\n    total_gen_mvar = sum(Q_g) * baseMVA\n\n    # Total load\n    total_load_mw = sum(b['pd'] for b in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n    total_load_mvar = sum(b['qd'] for b in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n\n    # Total losses\n    total_losses_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n    # Generator report\n    gen_report = []\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        gen_report.append({\n            'id': gen['id'],\n            'bus': gen['bus'],\n            'pg_MW': float(P_g[i] * baseMVA),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Q_g[i] * baseMVA),\n            'pmin_MW': float(gen['pmin'] * baseMVA),\n            'pmax_MW': float(gen['pmax'] * baseMVA),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(gen['qmin'] * baseMVA),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(gen['qmax'] * baseMVA),\n        })\n\n    # Bus report\n    bus_report = []\n    for bid in sorted(buses.keys()):\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        bus_report.append({\n            'id': bid,\n            'vm_pu': float(V_mag[i]),\n            'va_deg': float(V_ang[i]),\n            'vmin_pu': float(bus['vmin']),\n            'vmax_pu': float(bus['vmax']),\n        })\n\n    # Branch flows and loading\n    branch_loadings = []\n    for br in branches:\n        fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n        ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n\n        vm_f = V_mag[fi]\n        va_f = V_ang[fi] * np.pi / 180\n        vm_t = V_mag[ti]\n        va_t = V_ang[ti] * np.pi / 180\n\n        y = br['y']\n        g_ser = y.real\n        b_ser = y.imag\n        tap = br['tap']\n        tap_mag = abs(tap)\n        tap_ang = np.angle(tap)\n        theta_ij = va_f - va_t - tap_ang\n\n        # Forward flow\n        yc_f = br['yc_from']\n        b_shunt_f = yc_f.imag\n\n        P_ij = (g_ser / tap_mag**2) * vm_f**2 - (g_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) + b_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n        Q_ij = -(b_ser + b_shunt_f) / tap_mag**2 * vm_f**2 + (b_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) - g_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n        flow_from_mva = np.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2) * baseMVA\n\n        # Reverse flow\n        yc_t = br['yc_to']\n        b_shunt_t = yc_t.imag\n\n        P_ji = g_ser * vm_t**2 - (g_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) - b_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n        Q_ji = -(b_ser + b_shunt_t) * vm_t**2 - (b_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) + g_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n        flow_to_mva = np.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2) * baseMVA\n\n        limit_mva = br['rateA'] * baseMVA\n        loading_pct = max(flow_from_mva, flow_to_mva) / limit_mva * 100 if limit_mva > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_loadings.append({\n            'from_bus': br['from'],\n            'to_bus': br['to'],\n            'loading_pct': float(loading_pct),\n            'flow_from_MVA': float(flow_from_mva),\n            'flow_to_MVA': float(flow_to_mva),\n            'limit_MVA': float(limit_mva),\n        })\n\n    # Most loaded branches\n    branch_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_loadings[:10]\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    max_p_mismatch = 0.0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0.0\n\n    gen_at_bus = {}\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        if gen['bus'] not in gen_at_bus:\n            gen_at_bus[gen['bus']] = []\n        gen_at_bus[gen['bus']].append(i)\n\n    for bid in buses.keys():\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n\n        vm_i = V_mag[i]\n        va_i = V_ang[i] * np.pi / 180\n\n        # Generation\n        P_gen = sum(P_g[gi] for gi in gen_at_bus.get(bid, []))\n        Q_gen = sum(Q_g[gi] for gi in gen_at_bus.get(bid, []))\n\n        # Load and shunt\n        P_load = bus['pd']\n        Q_load = bus['qd']\n        P_shunt = bus['gs'] * vm_i**2\n        Q_shunt = -bus['bs'] * vm_i**2\n\n        # Branch flows\n        P_branch = 0\n        Q_branch = 0\n\n        for br in branches:\n            if br['from'] == bid or br['to'] == bid:\n                fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n                ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n\n                vm_f = V_mag[fi]\n                va_f = V_ang[fi] * np.pi / 180\n                vm_t = V_mag[ti]\n                va_t = V_ang[ti] * np.pi / 180\n\n                y = br['y']\n                g_ser = y.real\n                b_ser = y.imag\n                tap = br['tap']\n                tap_mag = abs(tap)\n                tap_ang = np.angle(tap)\n                theta_ij = va_f - va_t - tap_ang\n\n                if br['from'] == bid:\n                    yc_f = br['yc_from']\n                    b_shunt_f = yc_f.imag\n\n                    P_ij = (g_ser / tap_mag**2) * vm_f**2 - (g_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) + b_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n                    Q_ij = -(b_ser + b_shunt_f) / tap_mag**2 * vm_f**2 + (b_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) - g_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n                    P_branch += P_ij\n                    Q_branch += Q_ij\n\n                if br['to'] == bid:\n                    yc_t = br['yc_to']\n                    b_shunt_t = yc_t.imag\n\n                    P_ji = g_ser * vm_t**2 - (g_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) - b_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n                    Q_ji = -(b_ser + b_shunt_t) * vm_t**2 - (b_ser * np.cos(theta_ij) + g_ser * np.sin(theta_ij)) * vm_f * vm_t / tap_mag\n\n                    P_branch += P_ji\n                    Q_branch += Q_ji\n\n        p_mismatch = abs(P_gen - P_load - P_shunt - P_branch)\n        q_mismatch = abs(Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt - Q_branch)\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n    # Voltage violations\n    max_v_violation = 0.0\n    for bid in buses.keys():\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        if V_mag[i] < bus['vmin']:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, bus['vmin'] - V_mag[i])\n        elif V_mag[i] > bus['vmax']:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, V_mag[i] - bus['vmax'])\n\n    # Branch overloads\n    max_overload = 0.0\n    for bl in branch_loadings:\n        overload = max(bl['flow_from_MVA'], bl['flow_to_MVA']) - bl['limit_MVA']\n        if overload > 0:\n            max_overload = max(max_overload, overload)\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(solution['objective']),\n            'total_load_MW': float(total_load_mw),\n            'total_load_MVAr': float(total_load_mvar),\n            'total_generation_MW': float(total_gen_mw),\n            'total_generation_MVAr': float(total_gen_mvar),\n            'total_losses_MW': float(total_losses_mw),\n            'solver_status': solution['solver_status'],\n        },\n        'generators': gen_report,\n        'buses': bus_report,\n        'most_loaded_branches': most_loaded,\n        'feasibility_check': {\n            'max_p_mismatch_MW': float(max_p_mismatch * baseMVA),\n            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': float(max_q_mismatch * baseMVA),\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_v_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_overload),\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(\"AC Optimal Power Flow - Base Case Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    # {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /logs/agent/sessions/skills/ac-branch-pi-model\n\n# AC Branch Pi-Model + Transformer Handling\n\nImplement the exact branch power flow equations in `acopf-math-model.md` using MATPOWER branch data:\n\n```\n[F_BUS, T_BUS, BR_R, BR_X, BR_B, RATE_A, RATE_B, RATE_C, TAP, SHIFT, BR_STATUS, ANGMIN, ANGMAX]\n```\n\n## Quick start\n\n- Use `scripts/branch_flows.py` to compute per-unit branch flows.\n- Treat the results as **power leaving** the “from” bus and **power leaving** the “to” bus (i.e., compute both directions explicitly).\n\nExample:\n\n```python\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\n\nfrom scripts.branch_flows import compute_branch_flows_pu, build_bus_id_to_idx\n\ndata = json.load(open(\"/root/network.json\"))\nbaseMVA = float(data[\"baseMVA\"])\nbuses = np.array(data[\"bus\"], dtype=float)\nbranches = np.array(data[\"branch\"], dtype=float)\n\nbus_id_to_idx = build_bus_id_to_idx(buses)\n\nVm = buses[:, 7]  # initial guess VM\nVa = np.deg2rad(buses[:, 8])  # initial guess VA\n\nbr = branches[0]\nP_ij, Q_ij, P_ji, Q_ji = compute_branch_flows_pu(Vm, Va, br, bus_id_to_idx)\n\nS_ij_MVA = (P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2) ** 0.5 * baseMVA\nS_ji_MVA = (P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2) ** 0.5 * baseMVA\nprint(S_ij_MVA, S_ji_MVA)\n```\n\n## Model details (match the task formulation)\n\n### Per-unit conventions\n\n- Work in per-unit internally.\n- Convert with `baseMVA`:\n  - \\(P_{pu} = P_{MW} / baseMVA\\)\n  - \\(Q_{pu} = Q_{MVAr} / baseMVA\\)\n  - \\(|S|_{MVA} = |S|_{pu} \\cdot baseMVA\\)\n\n### Transformer handling (MATPOWER TAP + SHIFT)\n\n- Use \\(T_{ij} = tap \\cdot e^{j \\cdot shift}\\).\n- **Implementation shortcut (real tap + phase shift):**\n  - If `abs(TAP) < 1e-12`, treat `tap = 1.0` (no transformer).\n  - Convert `SHIFT` from degrees to radians.\n  - Use the angle shift by modifying the angle difference:\n    - \\(\\delta_{ij} = \\theta_i - \\theta_j - shift\\)\n    - \\(\\delta_{ji} = \\theta_j - \\theta_i + shift\\)\n\n### Series admittance\n\nGiven `BR_R = r`, `BR_X = x`:\n\n- If `r == 0 and x == 0`, set `g = 0`, `b = 0` (avoid divide-by-zero).\n- Else:\n  - \\(y = 1/(r + jx) = g + jb\\)\n  - \\(g = r/(r^2 + x^2)\\)\n  - \\(b = -x/(r^2 + x^2)\\)\n\n### Line charging susceptance\n\n- `BR_B` is the **total** line charging susceptance \\(b_c\\) (per unit).\n- Each end gets \\(b_c/2\\) in the standard pi model.\n\n## Power flow equations (use these exactly)\n\nLet:\n- \\(V_i = |V_i| e^{j\\theta_i}\\), \\(V_j = |V_j| e^{j\\theta_j}\\)\n- `tap` is real, `shift` is radians\n- `inv_t = 1/tap`, `inv_t2 = inv_t^2`\n\nThen the real/reactive power flow from i→j is:\n\n- \\(P_{ij} = g |V_i|^2 inv\\_t2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\cos\\delta_{ij} + b\\sin\\delta_{ij})\\)\n- \\(Q_{ij} = -(b + b_c/2)|V_i|^2 inv\\_t2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\sin\\delta_{ij} - b\\cos\\delta_{ij})\\)\n\nAnd from j→i is:\n\n- \\(P_{ji} = g |V_j|^2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\cos\\delta_{ji} + b\\sin\\delta_{ji})\\)\n- \\(Q_{ji} = -(b + b_c/2)|V_j|^2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\sin\\delta_{ji} - b\\cos\\delta_{ji})\\)\n\nCompute apparent power:\n\n- \\(|S_{ij}| = \\sqrt{P_{ij}^2 + Q_{ij}^2}\\)\n- \\(|S_{ji}| = \\sqrt{P_{ji}^2 + Q_{ji}^2}\\)\n\n## Common uses\n\n### Enforce MVA limits (rateA)\n\n- `RATE_A` is an MVA limit (may be 0 meaning “no limit”).\n- Enforce in both directions:\n  - \\(|S_{ij}| \\le RATE_A\\)\n  - \\(|S_{ji}| \\le RATE_A\\)\n\n### Compute branch loading %\n\nFor reporting “most loaded branches”:\n\n- `loading_pct = 100 * max(|S_ij|, |S_ji|) / RATE_A` if `RATE_A > 0`, else 0.\n\n### Aggregate bus injections for nodal balance\n\nTo build the branch flow sum for each bus \\(i\\):\n\n- Add \\(P_{ij}, Q_{ij}\\) to bus i\n- Add \\(P_{ji}, Q_{ji}\\) to bus j\n\nThis yields arrays `P_out[i]`, `Q_out[i]` such that the nodal balance can be written as:\n\n- \\(P^g - P^d - G^s|V|^2 = P_{out}\\)\n- \\(Q^g - Q^d + B^s|V|^2 = Q_{out}\\)\n\n## Sanity checks (fast debug)\n\n- With `SHIFT=0` and `TAP=1`, if \\(V_i = V_j\\) and \\(\\theta_i=\\theta_j\\), then \\(P_{ij}\\approx 0\\) and \\(P_{ji}\\approx 0\\) (lossless only if r=0).\n- For a pure transformer (`r=x=0`) you should not get meaningful flows; treat as `g=b=0` (no series element).\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"3b33a68c-e002-43bd-b0bc-83ce40604541","uuid":"aa13953c-dd47-4c8a-bc65-5659d3a0340a","isSynthetic":true}

Decision variables\n    V_mag = MX.sym('V_mag', n_bus)\n    V_ang = MX.sym('V_ang', n_bus)\n    P_g = MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)\n    Q_g = MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)\n\n    x = vertcat(V_mag, V_ang, P_g, Q_g)\n\n    # Objective\n    obj = 0\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        c2 = gen.get('c2', 0.0)\n        c1 = gen.get('c1', 1000.0)\n        c0 = gen.get('c0', 0.0)\n        pg_mw = P_g[i] * baseMVA\n        obj += c2 * pg_mw**2 + c1 * pg_mw + c0\n\n    # Constraints\n    g = []\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Build generator mapping\n    gen_at_bus = {bid: [] for bid in bus_ids}\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        gen_at_bus[gen['bus']].append(i)\n\n    # Power balance equations\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n\n        vm_i = V_mag[i]\n        va_i = V_ang[i]\n\n        # Generation at this bus\n        P_gen = MX(0)\n        Q_gen = MX(0)\n        for gi in gen_at_bus[bid]:\n            P_gen = P_gen + P_g[gi]\n            Q_gen = Q_gen + Q_g[gi]\n\n        # Load\n        P_load = MX(bus['pd'])\n        Q_load = MX(bus['qd'])\n\n        # Shunt\n        P_shunt = MX(bus['gs']) * vm_i**2\n        Q_shunt = -MX(bus['bs']) * vm_i**2\n\n        # Branch flows\n        P_branch = MX(0)\n        Q_branch = MX(0)\n\n        for br in branches:\n            if br['from'] == bid or br['to'] == bid:\n                fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n                ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n\n                vm_f = V_mag[fi]\n                va_f = V_ang[fi]\n                vm_t = V_mag[ti]\n                va_t = V_ang[ti]\n\n                g_ser = br['g']\n                b_ser = br['b']\n                bc = br['bc']\n                tap = br['tap']\n                shift = br['shift']\n\n                inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n                inv_t2 = inv_t * inv_t\n\n                if br['from'] == bid:\n                    # Forward flow i->j\n                    delta_ij = va_f - va_t - shift\n                    P_ij = g_ser * vm_f**2 * inv_t2 - vm_f * vm_t * inv_t * (g_ser * cos(delta_ij) + b_ser * sin(delta_ij))\n                    Q_ij = -(b_ser + bc/2.0) * vm_f**2 * inv_t2 - vm_f * vm_t * inv_t * (g_ser * sin(delta_ij) - b_ser * cos(delta_ij))\n\n                    P_branch = P_branch + P_ij\n                    Q_branch = Q_branch + Q_ij\n\n                if br['to'] == bid:\n                    # Reverse flow j->i\n                    delta_ji = va_t - va_f + shift\n                    P_ji = g_ser * vm_t**2 - vm_f * vm_t * inv_t * (g_ser * cos(delta_ji) + b_ser * sin(delta_ji))\n                    Q_ji = -(b_ser + bc/2.0) * vm_t**2 - vm_f * vm_t * inv_t * (g_ser * sin(delta_ji) - b_ser * cos(delta_ji))\n\n                    P_branch = P_branch + P_ji\n                    Q_branch = Q_branch + Q_ji\n\n        # Power balance constraints\n        g.append(P_gen - P_load - P_shunt - P_branch)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n        g.append(Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt - Q_branch)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Reference bus angle\n    ref_buses = [bid for bid in bus_ids if buses[bid]['type'] == 3]\n    if ref_buses:\n        ref_idx = bus_idx[ref_buses[0]]\n        g.append(V_ang[ref_idx])\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    for br in branches:\n        fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n        ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n\n        vm_f = V_mag[fi]\n        va_f = V_ang[fi]\n        vm_t = V_mag[ti]\n        va_t = V_ang[ti]\n\n        g_ser = br['g']\n        b_ser = br['b']\n        bc = br['bc']\n        tap = br['tap']\n        shift = br['shift']\n\n        inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n        inv_t2 = inv_t * inv_t\n\n        # Forward flow\n        delta_ij = va_f - va_t - shift\n        P_ij = g_ser * vm_f**2 * inv_t2 - vm_f * vm_t * inv_t * (g_ser * cos(delta_ij) + b_ser * sin(delta_ij))\n        Q_ij = -(b_ser + bc/2.0) * vm_f**2 * inv_t2 - vm_f * vm_t * inv_t * (g_ser * sin(delta_ij) - b_ser * cos(delta_ij))\n\n        S_ij_sq = P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2\n        h.append(S_ij_sq)\n        lbh.append(0)\n        ubh.append(br['rateA']**2)\n\n        # Reverse flow\n        delta_ji = va_t - va_f + shift\n        P_ji = g_ser * vm_t**2 - vm_f * vm_t * inv_t * (g_ser * cos(delta_ji) + b_ser * sin(delta_ji))\n        Q_ji = -(b_ser + bc/2.0) * vm_t**2 - vm_f * vm_t * inv_t * (g_ser * sin(delta_ji) - b_ser * cos(delta_ji))\n\n        S_ji_sq = P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2\n        h.append(S_ji_sq)\n        lbh.append(0)\n        ubh.append(br['rateA']**2)\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        lbx.append(bus['vmin'])\n        ubx.append(bus['vmax'])\n\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        lbx.append(-np.pi)\n        ubx.append(np.pi)\n\n    for gen in generators:\n        lbx.append(gen['pmin'])\n        ubx.append(gen['pmax'])\n\n    for gen in generators:\n        lbx.append(gen['qmin'])\n        ubx.append(gen['qmax'])\n\n    # Initial guess\n    x0 = []\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        x0.append(buses[bid]['vm'])\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        x0.append(buses[bid]['va'] * np.pi / 180.0)\n    for gen in generators:\n        x0.append(gen['pg'])\n    for gen in generators:\n        x0.append(gen['qg'])\n\n    # Setup NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': obj,\n        'g': vertcat(*g, *h) if (g and h) else (vertcat(*g) if g else vertcat(*h))\n    }\n\n    lbg_all = lbg + lbh\n    ubg_all = ubg + ubh\n\n    # Solver options\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'print_time': 0,\n    }\n\n    solver = nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving ACOPF...\")\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg_all, ubg=ubg_all)\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n\n    V_mag_opt = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n    V_ang_opt = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus] * 180 / np.pi\n    P_g_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Q_g_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    success = stats['success']\n\n    result = {\n        'success': success,\n        'solver_status': 'optimal' if success else 'infeasible',\n        'objective': float(sol['f']),\n        'bus_idx': bus_idx,\n        'V_mag': V_mag_opt,\n        'V_ang': V_ang_opt,\n        'P_g': P_g_opt,\n        'Q_g': Q_g_opt,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n    }\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef generate_report(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Generate the final report.json\"\"\"\n\n    buses = network['buses']\n    generators = network['generators']\n    branches = network['branches']\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    bus_idx = solution['bus_idx']\n    V_mag = solution['V_mag']\n    V_ang = solution['V_ang']\n    P_g = solution['P_g']\n    Q_g = solution['Q_g']\n\n    # Total generation\n    total_gen_mw = sum(P_g) * baseMVA\n    total_gen_mvar = sum(Q_g) * baseMVA\n\n    # Total load\n    total_load_mw = sum(b['pd'] for b in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n    total_load_mvar = sum(b['qd'] for b in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n\n    # Total losses\n    total_losses_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n    # Generator report\n    gen_report = []\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        gen_report.append({\n            'id': gen['id'],\n            'bus': gen['bus'],\n            'pg_MW': float(P_g[i] * baseMVA),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Q_g[i] * baseMVA),\n            'pmin_MW': float(gen['pmin'] * baseMVA),\n            'pmax_MW': float(gen['pmax'] * baseMVA),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(gen['qmin'] * baseMVA),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(gen['qmax'] * baseMVA),\n        })\n\n    # Bus report\n    bus_report = []\n    for bid in sorted(buses.keys()):\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        bus_report.append({\n            'id': bid,\n            'vm_pu': float(V_mag[i]),\n            'va_deg': float(V_ang[i]),\n            'vmin_pu': float(bus['vmin']),\n            'vmax_pu': float(bus['vmax']),\n        })\n\n    # Voltage violations\n    max_v_violation = 0.0\n    for bid in buses.keys():\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        if V_mag[i] < bus['vmin']:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, bus['vmin'] - V_mag[i])\n        elif V_mag[i] > bus['vmax']:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, V_mag[i] - bus['vmax'])\n\n    # Branch overloads\n    max_overload = 0.0\n    for bl in branch_loadings:\n        overload = max(bl['flow_from_MVA'], bl['flow_to_MVA']) - bl['limit_MVA']\n        if overload > 0:\n            max_overload = max(max_overload, overload)\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(solution['objective']),\n            'total_load_MW': float(total_load_mw),\n            'total_load_MVAr': float(total_load_mvar),\n            'total_generation_MW': float(total_gen_mw),\n            'total_generation_MVAr': float(total_gen_mvar),\n            'total_losses_MW': float(total_losses_mw),\n            'solver_status': solution['solver_status'],\n        },\n        'generators': gen_report,\n        'buses': bus_report,\n        'most_loaded_branches': most_loaded,\n        'feasibility_check': {\n            'max_p_mismatch_MW': float(max_p_mismatch * baseMVA),\n            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': float(max_q_mismatch * baseMVA),\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_v_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_overload),\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(\"AC Optimal Power Flow - Base Case Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\n    # Parse bus data\n    buses = {}\n    for row in data['bus']:\n        bus_id = int(row[0])\n        buses[bus_id] = {\n            'id': bus_id,\n            'type': int(row[1]),\n            'pd': row[2] / baseMVA,\n            'qd': row[3] / baseMVA,\n            'gs': row[4] / baseMVA,\n            'bs': row[5] / baseMVA,\n            'vm': row[7],\n            'va': row[8],\n            'vmax': row[11],\n            'vmin': row[12],\n        }\n\n    # Parse generator data\n    generators = []\n    for idx, row in enumerate(data['gen']):\n        bus_id = int(row[0])\n        gen = {\n            'id': idx,\n            'bus': bus_id,\n            'pg': row[1] / baseMVA,\n            'qg': row[2] / baseMVA,\n            'qmax': row[3] / baseMVA,\n            'qmin': row[4] / baseMVA,\n            'vg': row[5],\n            'mbase': row[6],\n            'status': int(row[7]),\n            'pmax': row[8] / baseMVA,\n            'pmin': row[9] / baseMVA,\n        }\n        if gen['status'] == 1:\n            generators.append(gen)\n\n    # Parse generator cost data\n    for idx, cost_row in enumerate(data['gencost']):\n        if idx < len(generators):\n            model = int(cost_row[0])\n            ncost = int(cost_row[3])\n            if model == 2 and ncost == 3:\n                generators[idx]['c2'] = cost_row[4] * baseMVA**2\n                generators[idx]['c1'] = cost_row[5] * baseMVA\n                generators[idx]['c0'] = cost_row[6]\n            else:\n                generators[idx]['c2'] = 0.0\n                generators[idx]['c1'] = cost_row[-2] * baseMVA if len(cost_row) > 5 else 1000.0\n                generators[idx]['c0'] = cost_row[-1] if len(cost_row) > 6 else 0.0\n\n    # Parse branch data\n    branches = []\n    for row in data['branch']:\n        fbus = int(row[0])\n        tbus = int(row[1])\n        r = row[2]\n        x = row[3]\n        bc = row[4]  # Total line charging susceptance\n\n        # Series admittance\n        if abs(r) < 1e-12 and abs(x) < 1e-12:\n            g = 0.0\n            b = 0.0\n        else:\n            denom = r * r + x * x\n            g = r / denom\n            b = -x / denom\n\n        # Transformer\n        tap = row[8] if abs(row[8]) > 1e-12 else 1.0\n        shift = row[9] * np.pi / 180.0  # degrees to radians\n\n        rateA = row[5] / baseMVA if row[5] > 0 else 9900.0\n        status = int(row[10])\n\n        branch = {\n            'from': fbus,\n            'to': tbus,\n            'g': g,\n            'b': b,\n            'bc': bc,\n            'tap': tap,\n            'shift': shift,\n            'rateA': rateA,\n            'status': status,\n        }\n\n        if branch['status'] == 1:\n            branches.append(branch)\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'generators': generators,\n        'branches': branches,\n    }\n\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve ACOPF using corrected polar formulation\"\"\"\n\n    buses = network['buses']\n    generators = network['generators']\n    branches = network['branches']\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    bus_ids = sorted(buses.keys())\n    bus_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n    n_bus = len(bus_ids)\n    n_gen = len(generators)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    print(f\"Setting up ACOPF: {n_bus} buses, {n_gen} generators, {n_branch} branches\")\n\n    # Decision variables\n    V_mag = MX.sym('V_mag', n_bus)\n    V_ang = MX.sym('V_ang', n_bus)\n    P_g = MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)\n    Q_g = MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)\n\n    x = vertcat(V_mag, V_ang, P_g, Q_g)\n\n    # Objective\n    obj = 0\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        c2 = gen.get('c2', 0.0)\n        c1 = gen.get('c1', 1000.0)\n        c0 = gen.get('c0', 0.0)\n        pg_mw = P_g[i] * baseMVA\n        obj += c2 * pg_mw**2 + c1 * pg_mw + c0\n\n    # Constraints\n    g = []\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Build generator mapping\n    gen_at_bus = {bid: [] for bid in bus_ids}\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        gen_at_bus[gen['bus']].append(i)\n\n    # Power balance equations\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        i = bus_idx[bid]\n        bus = buses[bid]\n\n        vm_i = V_mag[i]\n        va_i = V_ang[i]\n\n        # Generation at this bus\n        P_gen = MX(0)\n        Q_gen = MX(0)\n        for gi in gen_at_bus[bid]:\n            P_gen = P_gen + P_g[gi]\n            Q_gen = Q_gen + Q_g[gi]\n\n        # Load\n        P_load = MX(bus['pd'])\n        Q_load = MX(bus['qd'])\n\n        # Shunt\n        P_shunt = MX(bus['gs']) * vm_i**2\n        Q_shunt = -MX(bus['bs']) * vm_i**2\n\n        # Branch flows\n        P_branch = MX(0)\n        Q_branch = MX(0)\n\n        for br in branches:\n            if br['from'] == bid or br['to'] == bid:\n                fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n                ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n\n                vm_f = V_mag[fi]\n                va_f = V_ang[fi]\n                vm_t = V_mag[ti]\n                va_t = V_ang[ti]\n\n                g_ser = br['g']\n                b_ser = br['b']\n                bc = br['bc']\n                tap = br['tap']\n                shift = br['shift']\n\n                inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n                inv_t2 = inv_t * inv_t\n\n                if br['from'] == bid:\n                    # Forward flow i->j\n                    delta_ij = va_f - va_t - shift\n                    P_ij = g_ser * vm_f**2 * inv_t2 - vm_f * vm_t * inv_t * (g_ser * cos(delta_ij) + b_ser * sin(delta_ij))\n                    Q_ij = -(b_ser + bc/2.0) * vm_f**2 * inv_t2 - vm_f * vm_t * inv_t * (g_ser * sin(delta_ij) - b_ser * cos(delta_ij))\n\n                    P_branch = P_branch + P_ij\n                    Q_branch = Q_branch + Q_ij\n\n                if br['to'] == bid:\n                    # Reverse flow j->i\n                    delta_ji = va_t - va_f + shift\n                    P_ji = g_ser * vm_t**2 - vm_f * vm_t * inv_t * (g_ser * cos(delta_ji) + b_ser * sin(delta_ji))\n                    Q_ji = -(b_ser + bc/2.0) * vm_t**2 - vm_f * vm_t * inv_t * (g_ser * sin(delta_ji) - b_ser * cos(delta_ji))\n\n                    P_branch = P_branch + P_ji\n                    Q_branch = Q_branch + Q_ji\n\n        # Power balance constraints\n        g.append(P_gen - P_load - P_shunt - P_branch)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n        g.append(Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt - Q_branch)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Reference bus angle\n    ref_buses = [bid for bid in bus_ids if buses[bid]['type'] == 3]\n    if ref_buses:\n        ref_idx = bus_idx[ref_buses[0]]\n        g.append(V_ang[ref_idx])\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    for br in branches:\n        fi = bus_idx[br['from']]\n        ti = bus_idx[br['to']]\n\n        vm_f = V_mag[fi]\n        va_f = V_ang[fi]\n        vm_t = V_mag[ti]\n        va_t = V_ang[ti]\n\n        g_ser = br['g']\n        b_ser = br['b']\n        bc = br['bc']\n        tap = br['tap']\n        shift = br['shift']\n\n        inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n        inv_t2 = inv_t * inv_t\n\n        # Forward flow\n        delta_ij = va_f - va_t - shift\n        P_ij = g_ser * vm_f**2 * inv_t2 - vm_f * vm_t * inv_t * (g_ser * cos(delta_ij) + b_ser * sin(delta_ij))\n        Q_ij = -(b_ser + bc/2.0) * vm_f**2 * inv_t2 - vm_f * vm_t * inv_t * (g_ser * sin(delta_ij) - b_ser * cos(delta_ij))\n\n        S_ij_sq = P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2\n        h.append(S_ij_sq)\n        lbh.append(0)\n        ubh.append(br['rateA']**2)\n\n        # Reverse flow\n        delta_ji = va_t - va_f + shift\n        P_ji = g_ser * vm_t**2 - vm_f * vm_t * inv_t * (g_ser * cos(delta_ji) + b_ser * sin(delta_ji))\n        Q_ji = -(b_ser + bc/2.0) * vm_t**2 - vm_f * vm_t * inv_t * (g_ser * sin(delta_ji) - b_ser * cos(delta_ji))\n\n        S_ji_sq = P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2\n        h.append(S_ji_sq)\n        lbh.append(0)\n        ubh.append(br['rateA']**2)\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        bus = buses[bid]\n        lbx.append(bus['vmin'])\n        ubx.append(bus['vmax'])\n\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        lbx.append(-np.pi)\n        ubx.append(np.pi)\n\n    for gen in generators:\n        lbx.append(gen['pmin'])\n        ubx.append(gen['pmax'])\n\n    for gen in generators:\n        lbx.append(gen['qmin'])\n        ubx.append(gen['qmax'])\n\n    # Initial guess\n    x0 = []\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        x0.append(buses[bid]['vm'])\n    for bid in bus_ids:\n        x0.append(buses[bid]['va'] * np.pi / 180.0)\n    for gen in generators:\n        x0.append(gen['pg'])\n    for gen in generators:\n        x0.append(gen['qg'])\n\n    # Setup NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': obj,\n        'g': vertcat(*g, *h) if (g and h) else (vertcat(*g) if g else vertcat(*h))\n    }\n\n    lbg_all = lbg + lbh\n    ubg_all = ubg + ubh\n\n    # Solver options\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'print_time': 0,\n    }\n\n    solver = nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving ACOPF...\")\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg_all, ubg=ubg_all)\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n\n    V_mag_opt = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n    V_ang_opt = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus] * 180 / np.pi\n    P_g_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Q_g_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    success = stats['success']\n\n    result = {\n        'success': success,\n        'solver_status': 'optimal' if success else 'infeasible',\n        'objective': float(sol['f']),\n        'bus_idx': bus_idx,\n        'V_mag': V_mag_opt,\n        'V_ang': V_ang_opt,\n        'P_g': P_g_opt,\n        'Q_g': Q_g_opt,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n    }\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef generate_report(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Generate the final report.json\"\"\"\n\n    buses = network['buses']\n    generators = network['generators']\n    branches = network['branches']\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n\n    bus_idx = solution['bus_idx']\n    V_mag = solution['V_mag']\n    V_ang = solution['V_ang']\n    P_g = solution['P_g']\n    Q_g = solution['Q_g']\n\n    # Total generation\n    total_gen_mw = sum(P_g) * baseMVA\n    total_gen_mvar = sum(Q_g) * baseMVA\n\n    # Total load\n    total_load_mw = sum(b['pd'] for b in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n    total_load_mvar = sum(b['qd'] for b in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n\n    # Total losses\n    total_losses_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n    # Generator report\n    gen_report = []\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        gen_report.append({\n            'id': gen['id'],\n            'bus': gen['bus'],\n            'pg_MW': float(P_g[i] * baseMVA),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Q_g[i] * baseMVA),\n            'pmin_MW': float(gen['pmin'] * baseMVA),\n            'pmax_MW': float(gen['pmax'] * baseMVA),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(gen['qmin'] * baseMVA),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(gen['qmax'] * baseMVA),\n        })\n\n    #
